  "short_description": {
    "id": "Odin adalah dewa utama dalam mitologi Nordik yang digambarkan bijak dan bermata satu, dan terkait dengan perang, puisi, orang mati, rune, dan sihir.",
    "en": "Odin is the main god of Norse mythology, depicted as wise and one-eyed and associated with war, poetry, the dead, runes, and magic.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "odin-c01",
      "odin-c02",
      "odin-c19"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Odin adalah dewa utama dalam mitologi Nordik. Ia digambarkan sebagai lelaki tua yang sangat bijak dan bermata satu, dikenali dari topi dan jubahnya, janggut panjang, serta tombak Gungnir. Menurut sumber, ia tinggal di Asgard, benteng para dewa. Cincin Draupnir dan kuda berkaki delapan Sleipnir juga termasuk atributnya, dan dua gagaknya, Huginn dan Muninn, terbang berkeliling dunia mengumpulkan kabar lalu membisikkannya ke telinganya.",
      "en": "Odin is the main god of Norse mythology. He is depicted as an immensely wise, one-eyed old man, recognised by his hat and cloak, long beard, and spear Gungnir. According to the sources he resides in Asgard, the stronghold of the gods. The ring Draupnir and the eight-legged horse Sleipnir are also among his attributes, and his two ravens, Huginn and Muninn, fly around the world gathering news and whisper it into his ear.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "odin-c01",
        "odin-c08",
        "odin-c09",
        "odin-c10",
        "odin-c34",
        "odin-c03",
        "odin-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ranah Odin mencakup puisi, orang mati, rune, dan sihir, serta perang: dalam sumber Jermanik selatan dan barat, Odin yang menentukan apakah pertempuran atau prajurit tertentu menang. Para Valkyrie membawa prajurit yang gugur ke Valhalla; mereka dikenal sebagai Einherjar dan menjadi pasukan Odin melawan kekuatan dunia bawah saat Ragnarök. Odin juga digambarkan sebagai pemimpin Wild Hunt, pasukan orang mati yang menunggang badai. Sihirnya membuatnya sering dipandang sebagai syaman, dan ia juga memiliki fungsi sebagai dewa penyembuhan, dan ia memakai sihir untuk melihat sekilas masa depan.",
      "en": "Odin's domain covers poetry, the dead, runes, and magic, as well as war: in southern and western Germanic sources it is Odin who decides whether battles or individual warriors are victorious. The Valkyries bring slain fighters to Valhalla; these fighters, known as the Einherjar, become Odin's strike-force against the powers of the Underworld at Ragnarök. Odin is also portrayed as the leader of the Wild Hunt, an army of the dead who ride on the storms. His magic often leads him to be seen as a shaman and a god of healing, and he uses magic to catch glimpses of the future.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "odin-c02",
        "odin-c19",
        "odin-c18",
        "odin-c06",
        "odin-c17",
        "odin-c28",
        "odin-c32"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Kisah-kisah Odin banyak berkisah tentang pencarian pengetahuan. Menurut Edda Prosa, ia baru boleh minum dari sumur Mimer setelah meninggalkan salah satu matanya sebagai jaminan. Ia juga menggantung sembilan malam di Yggdrasil dengan luka tombak sebelum rune terungkap kepadanya, dan mencuri mead para skald lalu kabur dalam wujud elang. Dalam kisah penciptaan, Odin dan saudara-saudaranya Vili dan Vé membunuh raksasa purba Ymir dan membentuk bumi, langit, gunung, dan laut dari tubuhnya.",
      "en": "Many of Odin's stories concern the search for knowledge. According to the Prose Edda, he was allowed to drink from Mimer's well only after leaving one of his eyes there as a pledge. He also hangs for nine nights on Yggdrasil with a spear wound before the runes are revealed to him, and he steals the mead of the skalds and escapes in the shape of an eagle. In the creation story, Odin and his brothers Vili and Vé slay the proto-giant Ymir and make the earth, sky, mountains, and sea from his body.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "odin-c12",
        "odin-c13",
        "odin-c14",
        "odin-c33",
        "odin-c15",
        "odin-c16",
        "odin-c11"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pada Ragnarök, Odin maju paling depan dengan helm emas, zirah gemerlap, dan tombak Gungner melawan serigala Fenris, dan ia terbunuh oleh Fenrir. Para pemuja utamanya sebagian besar kalangan elite, yaitu raja, kepala suku, dan penyair, serta prajurit. Adam dari Bremen menulis pada 1070 M bahwa di kuil Uppsala kuno persembahan untuk Odin dibuat pada masa perang. Kisah Odin dikenal antara lain dari Edda dan saga Islandia yang ditulis pada Abad Pertengahan.",
      "en": "At Ragnarök, Odin rides first with his golden helmet, resplendent byrnie, and spear Gungner against the Fenris-wolf, and he is killed by Fenrir. His main worshippers were mostly the elite, namely kings, chieftains, and poets, along with warriors. Adam of Bremen wrote in 1070 CE that at the Old Uppsala temple sacrifices were made to Odin in times of war. The tales of Odin are known from Edda writings and Icelandic sagas recorded in the Middle Ages.",

      "title": {
        "id": "Sumur Mimer dan mata Odin",
        "en": "Mimer's well and Odin's eye"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Pencari pengetahuan yang menyerahkan matanya",
        "en": "Seeker of knowledge who gives up an eye"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Menurut Edda Prosa, Alfather datang ke sumur Mimer untuk minum, tetapi baru mendapatkannya setelah meninggalkan salah satu matanya sebagai jaminan. World History Encyclopedia menyebut pengetahuan Odin berasal dari kepala Mímir atau dari minum air sumur Mímir.",
        "en": "According to the Prose Edda, Alfather came to Mimer's well to drink but received a drink only after leaving one of his eyes as a pledge. World History Encyclopedia says Odin's knowledge comes from Mímir's head or from drinking from Mímir's well."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "odin-c12",
        "odin-c11"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Pengorbanan diri demi rune",
        "en": "Self-sacrifice for the runes"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Orang yang berkorban",
        "en": "The one who sacrifices himself"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Selama sembilan malam Odin tergantung pada sebuah dahan Yggdrasil, pohon dunia, dengan luka tombak yang hampir merenggut nyawanya; baru setelah itu rune tersingkap baginya.",
        "en": "For nine nights Odin dangles from a bough of the World Tree Yggdrasil, wounded by a spear and close to death, and only afterwards are the runes revealed to him."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "odin-c13"

    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Mead para skald",
        "en": "The mead of the skalds"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Pencuri mead",
        "en": "The thief of the mead"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Menurut Snorri, kemahiran puisi Odin berasal dari pencurian mead para skald setelah ia tidur tiga malam dengan raksasa perempuan Gunnlǫð. Ia diizinkan tiga tegukan, menghabiskan semua wadah, lalu berubah menjadi elang dan kabur.",
        "en": "According to Snorri, Odin's poetic skill comes from stealing the mead of the skalds after sleeping for three nights with the giantess Gunnlǫð. He is allowed three sips, drains the containers, then shape-shifts into an eagle and flees."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "odin-c14",
        "odin-c33"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Penciptaan dunia",
        "en": "The creation of the world"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Pencipta bersama saudara-saudaranya",
        "en": "Creator together with his brothers"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Borr dan Bestla menjadi orang tua Odin dan saudara-saudaranya, yang biasanya bernama Vili dan Vé. Mereka membunuh raksasa purba Ymir dan membentuk bumi dari dagingnya, langit dari tengkoraknya, gunung dari tulangnya, dan laut dari darahnya.",
        "en": "Borr and Bestla sired Odin and his brothers, usually named Vili and Vé. They slew the proto-giant Ymir and made the earth from his flesh, the sky from his skull, the mountains from his bones, and the sea from his blood."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "odin-c15",
        "odin-c16"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Adu pengetahuan Vafþrúðnismál dan Grímnismál",
        "en": "The contests of knowledge in Vafþrúðnismál and Grímnismál"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Peserta adu kecerdasan dan penyamar",
        "en": "Contestant in the battle of wits and the disguised wanderer"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Dalam Vafþrúðnismál Odin memulai adu kecerdasan dengan raksasa Vafþrúðnir dan mengalahkannya. Dalam Grímnismál Odin yang menyamar disiksa dan akhirnya membuka pengetahuannya kepada Raja Geirroðr.",
        "en": "In Vafþrúðnismál Odin starts a battle of wits with the giant Vafþrúðnir and beats him. In Grímnismál a disguised Odin is tortured and ends up revealing his knowledge to King Geirroðr."
      },

        "en": "In the Prose Edda Odin rides first with a golden helmet, resplendent byrnie, and the spear Gungner against the Fenris-wolf. In the mythic future he leads the einherjar and is killed by Fenrir."

  "learning_questions": [
    {
      "id": "Mengapa para penulis kemudian mungkin menggambarkan Odin sebagai Allfather meskipun kedudukannya di masyarakat Zaman Viking berbeda?",
      "en": "Why might later writers have portrayed Odin as Allfather even though his standing in Viking Age societies differed?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Pengorbanan apa saja yang dilakukan Odin untuk mendapatkan pengetahuan, dan apa yang diperolehnya?",
      "en": "What sacrifices does Odin make to gain knowledge, and what does he obtain?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Bagaimana atribut seperti gagak dan tombak membantu kita mengenali Odin dalam kisah dan hiasan?",
      "en": "How do attributes such as the ravens and the spear help us recognise Odin in stories and ornaments?"
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "odin-c01",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin (Old Norse: Óðinn) is the main god in Norse mythology. Described as an immensely wise, one-eyed old man",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin adalah dewa utama mitologi Nordik yang digambarkan bijak dan bermata satu.",
        "en": "Odin is a leading Norse god depicted as wise and one-eyed."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c02",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "the god of poetry, of the dead, of runes, and of magic.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin terkait dengan puisi, orang mati, rune, dan sihir.",
        "en": "Odin is associated with poetry, the dead, runes, and magic."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c03",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin's two ravens, Huginn ('thought') and Muninn ('wisdom') are very old mythical elements",
      "locator": "Attributes",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dua gagak Odin bernama Huginn dan Muninn.",
        "en": "Odin's two ravens are Huginn and Muninn."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c04",
      "source_id": "odin-s2",
      "quote": "In the mythic future, Odin leads the einherjar at Ragnarök, where he is killed by the monstrous wolf Fenrir.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Ragnarök Odin dibunuh oleh Fenrir.",
        "en": "Odin is killed by Fenrir at Ragnarök."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c05",
      "source_id": "odin-s3",
      "quote": "From written sources such as Snorri's Edda, other Edda poems and the Icelandic sagas, which was written down in the Middle Ages, we know the stories of Odin, Thor and the other Norse gods.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah Odin antara lain dikenal dari Edda dan saga Islandia yang ditulis pada Abad Pertengahan.",
        "en": "Odin's tales survive in Edda writings and Icelandic sagas recorded in the Middle Ages."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c06",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "gathers slain warriors around him in Valhalla ('hall of the slain')",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin mengumpulkan pejuang gugur di Valhalla.",
        "en": "Odin gathers fallen warriors in Valhalla."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c07",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "They fly around the world gathering news, and when they return, they sit on Odin's shoulder and whisper their tidings into his ear.",
      "locator": "Attributes",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Huginn dan Muninn terbang mencari kabar bagi Odin.",
        "en": "The ravens fly to gather news for Odin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c08",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin can be recognised by his hat and cloak, long beard, and only one eye",
      "locator": "Attributes",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin dikenali dari topi dan jubahnya, janggut panjang, dan hanya satu mata.",
        "en": "Odin is recognised by his hat and cloak, long beard, and only one eye."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c09",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "His spear Gungnir is one of his main attributes",
      "locator": "Attributes",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tombak Gungnir adalah salah satu atribut utama Odin.",
        "en": "His spear Gungnir is one of his main attributes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c10",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Some other attributes are the ring Draupnir, which drips to form eight new rings every nine nights, and the horse Sleipnir, which is mentioned early on in Old Norse literature and is set apart by the somehow efficient total of eight legs.",
      "locator": "Attributes",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Atribut Odin lainnya adalah cincin Draupnir, yang meneteskan delapan cincin baru setiap sembilan malam, dan kuda Sleipnir yang berkaki delapan.",
        "en": "Other attributes of Odin are the ring Draupnir, which drips eight new rings every nine nights, and the eight-legged horse Sleipnir."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c11",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin's immense knowledge is courtesy of Mímir's head (or else due to drinking from Mímir's well).",
      "locator": "Myths Involving Odin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pengetahuan Odin yang luas disebut berasal dari kepala Mímir, atau dari minum air sumur Mímir.",
        "en": "Odin's immense knowledge is said to come from Mímir's head, or else from drinking from Mímir's well."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c12",
      "source_id": "odin-s4",
      "quote": "The owner of the well hight Mimer. He is full of wisdom, for he drinks from the well with the Gjallar-horn. Alfather once came there and asked for a drink from the well, but he did not get it before he left one of his eyes as a pledge.",
      "locator": "Gylfaginning, the well of Mimer",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Edda Prosa, Alfather tidak mendapat minum dari sumur Mimer sebelum meninggalkan salah satu matanya sebagai jaminan.",
        "en": "According to the Prose Edda, Alfather received a drink from Mimer's well only after leaving one of his eyes there as a pledge."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c13",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "nine nights of swinging from a branch of Yggdrasil, the World Tree, with a spear wound that put him close to death, and only then are the runes revealed to him.",
      "locator": "Myths Involving Odin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin menggantung sembilan malam di dahan Yggdrasil, pohon dunia, dengan luka tombak yang nyaris membunuhnya, dan baru setelah itu rune terungkap kepadanya.",
        "en": "Odin hangs for nine nights from a branch of Yggdrasil, the World Tree, with a spear wound that brings him close to death, and only then are the runes revealed to him."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c14",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Snorri explains that Odin's superb poetic skills are due to him stealing the mead of the skalds (i.e. poets) by sleeping with the giantess Gunnlǫð, guardian of the mead, for three nights.",
      "locator": "Myths Involving Odin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Snorri menjelaskan bahwa kemahiran puisi Odin berasal dari pencurian mead para skald (penyair) setelah ia tidur tiga malam dengan raksasa perempuan Gunnlǫð, penjaga mead itu.",
        "en": "Snorri explains that Odin's poetic skill comes from stealing the mead of the skalds (poets) after sleeping for three nights with the giantess Gunnlǫð, the mead's guardian."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c15",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "his son Borr and the giant-daughter Bestla sired Odin and his brothers (usually named Vili and Vé)",
      "locator": "Myths Involving Odin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Borr dan raksasa perempuan Bestla menjadi orang tua Odin dan saudara-saudaranya, yang biasanya bernama Vili dan Vé.",
        "en": "Borr and the giantess Bestla sired Odin and his brothers, usually named Vili and Vé."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c16",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "The brothers then slew the proto-giant Ymir and used his flesh to create the earth, his skull to form the sky, his bones for the mountains, and his blood to make the sea.",
      "locator": "Myths Involving Odin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin bersaudara membunuh raksasa purba Ymir dan memakai dagingnya untuk bumi, tengkoraknya untuk langit, tulangnya untuk gunung, dan darahnya untuk laut.",
        "en": "Odin and his brothers slew the proto-giant Ymir and made the earth from his flesh, the sky from his skull, the mountains from his bones, and the sea from his blood."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c17",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin's connection with war flows naturally into a connection with the dead, which is further illustrated by Odin as the leader of the so-called Wild Hunt - a widespread and old Germanic cult centred around a myth of an army of the dead who ride on the storms.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hubungan Odin dengan perang berlanjut ke hubungannya dengan orang mati, antara lain sebagai pemimpin Perburuan Liar (Wild Hunt), pasukan orang mati yang menunggang badai.",
        "en": "Odin's link with war extends to a link with the dead, shown in his role as leader of the Wild Hunt, an army of the dead who ride on the storms."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c18",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin has his Valkyries - supernatural warrior women - bringing the bodies of fighters slain in battle to his special warrior paradise Valhalla; these fighters are known as the Einherjar and become Odin's strike-force against the encroaching powers of the Underworld during the Ragnarök.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para Valkyrie, perempuan prajurit gaib, membawa jasad prajurit yang gugur ke Valhalla; mereka dikenal sebagai Einherjar dan menjadi pasukan Odin melawan kekuatan dunia bawah saat Ragnarök.",
        "en": "Odin's Valkyries, supernatural warrior women, bring fighters slain in battle to Valhalla; these fighters, the Einherjar, become Odin's strike-force against the powers of the Underworld at Ragnarök."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c19",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "In southern and western Germanic sources, it was Odin who decided whether battles or individual warriors would be victorious or end in slightly less fortunate ways.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sumber Jermanik selatan dan barat, Odin yang menentukan kemenangan pertempuran atau prajurit tertentu.",
        "en": "In southern and western Germanic sources, Odin decided whether battles or individual warriors would be victorious."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c20",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "He [Odin] is described as falling into trances, as leaving his body behind and travelling abroad in the spirit-form of an animal, and several times as having visions of wisdom provoked by various kinds of ordeal.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin digambarkan jatuh ke dalam trans, meninggalkan tubuhnya dan mengembara dalam wujud roh hewan, serta beberapa kali mendapat penglihatan kebijaksanaan lewat berbagai cobaan.",
        "en": "Odin is described as falling into trances, leaving his body to travel in the spirit-form of an animal, and several times having visions of wisdom provoked by ordeals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c21",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "in the great pagan temple of Old Uppsala in Sweden, too; in 1070 CE Adam of Bremen wrote of his visit to this place that a statue of Thor stood prominently in the centre of the hall, with Odin and Freyr to the side, and that sacrifices were made to Thor in case of famine, to Odin in times of war",
      "locator": "Cult of Odin",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 1070 M, Adam dari Bremen menulis bahwa di kuil Uppsala kuno patung Thor berdiri di tengah, Odin dan Freyr di sisinya, dan persembahan untuk Odin dibuat pada masa perang.",
        "en": "In 1070 CE Adam of Bremen wrote that at the Old Uppsala temple a statue of Thor stood in the centre with Odin and Freyr at the sides, and that sacrifices were made to Odin in times of war."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c22",
      "source_id": "odin-s2",
      "quote": "Several of these stem from the reconstructed Proto-Germanic theonym Wōðanaz, meaning \"lord of frenzy\" or \"leader of the possessed\"",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Beberapa nama dan gelar Odin berasal dari nama dewa Proto-Jermanik hasil rekonstruksi, Wōðanaz, yang berarti \"penguasa kegilaan\" atau \"pemimpin orang yang kerasukan\".",
        "en": "Several of Odin's names and titles stem from the reconstructed Proto-Germanic theonym Wōðanaz, meaning \"lord of frenzy\" or \"leader of the possessed\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c23",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "He also exists in Germanic mythology as Woden (in Old English), Wodan (in Old Franconian), and Wutan or Wuotan (in Old High German).",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin juga dikenal dalam mitologi Jermanik sebagai Woden (Inggris Kuno), Wodan (Franka Kuno), serta Wutan atau Wuotan (Jerman Kuno Tinggi).",
        "en": "Odin also exists in Germanic mythology as Woden (Old English), Wodan (Old Franconian), and Wutan or Wuotan (Old High German)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c24",
      "source_id": "odin-s4",
      "quote": "Alfather he is called in our tongue, but in Asgard of old he had twelve names.",
      "locator": "Gylfaginning, chapter 3",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Edda Prosa, sang dewa tertinggi disebut Alfather, dan di Asgard lama ia memiliki dua belas nama.",
        "en": "According to the Prose Edda, the highest god is called Alfather, and in old Asgard he had twelve names."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c25",
      "source_id": "odin-s4",
      "quote": "Odin rides first; with his golden helmet, resplendent byrnie, and his spear Gungner, he advances against the Fenris-wolf.",
      "locator": "Gylfaginning, chapter 56",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Edda Prosa, Odin maju paling depan dengan helm emas, baju zirah gemerlap, dan tombak Gungner melawan serigala Fenris.",
        "en": "In the Prose Edda, Odin rides first, with golden helmet, resplendent byrnie, and his spear Gungner, and advances against the Fenris-wolf."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c26",
      "source_id": "odin-s2",
      "quote": "He was also adapted in Neil Gaiman's 2001 novel American Gods as Mr. Wednesday, a con artist revealed to be an incarnation of Odin.",
      "locator": "Modern influence",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin diadaptasi dalam novel American Gods karya Neil Gaiman (2001) sebagai Mr. Wednesday, penipu yang ternyata adalah inkarnasi Odin.",
        "en": "Odin was adapted in Neil Gaiman's 2001 novel American Gods as Mr. Wednesday, a con artist revealed to be an incarnation of Odin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c27",
      "source_id": "odin-s2",
      "quote": "Odin was adapted as a character by Marvel Comics, first appearing in the Journey into Mystery series in 1962.",
      "locator": "Modern influence",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin diadaptasi sebagai tokoh oleh Marvel Comics dan pertama muncul dalam seri Journey into Mystery pada 1962.",
        "en": "Odin was adapted as a character by Marvel Comics, first appearing in the Journey into Mystery series in 1962."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c28",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "The nature of Odin's magic moreover often causes him to be seen as a shaman, an aspect which is further bolstered by his function as a god of healing.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sihir Odin membuatnya sering dipandang sebagai syaman, dan pandangan ini diperkuat oleh fungsinya sebagai dewa penyembuhan.",
        "en": "The nature of Odin's magic often leads him to be seen as a shaman, an aspect reinforced by his function as a god of healing."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c29",
      "source_id": "odin-s2",
      "quote": "Wider sources depict Odin as the son of Bestla and Borr; brother to Vili and Vé; and husband to the goddess Frigg, with whom he fathered Baldr. Odin has many other sons, including Thor, whom he sired with the earth-goddess Jörð.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sumber yang lebih luas menggambarkan Odin sebagai putra Bestla dan Borr, saudara Vili dan Vé, serta suami dewi Frigg, yang melahirkan Baldr; ia juga ayah Thor dari dewi bumi Jörð.",
        "en": "Wider sources depict Odin as son of Bestla and Borr, brother of Vili and Vé, and husband of the goddess Frigg, with whom he fathered Baldr; he also fathered Thor with the earth-goddess Jörð."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c30",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin's role of 'Allfather' or father of the gods is more of a literary theme from later sources - seemingly influenced by Christian names for God - than an actual reflection of his status in Viking Age societies.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut World History Encyclopedia, peran Odin sebagai Allfather lebih merupakan tema sastra dari sumber yang lebih muda, tampaknya dipengaruhi sebutan Kristen untuk Tuhan, ketimbang cerminan kedudukannya di masyarakat Zaman Viking.",
        "en": "According to World History Encyclopedia, Odin's role as Allfather is more a literary theme from later sources, seemingly influenced by Christian names for God, than a reflection of his status in Viking Age societies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c31",
      "source_id": "odin-s4",
      "quote": "And for this reason Odin is called Alfather, that he is the father of all gods and men, and of all things that were made by him and by his might.",
      "locator": "Gylfaginning, chapter 9",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Edda Prosa, Odin disebut Alfather karena ia bapak semua dewa dan manusia serta segala yang dibuat olehnya.",
        "en": "According to the Prose Edda, Odin is called Alfather because he is the father of all gods and men and of all things made by him and his might."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c32",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin used mainly magic to catch glimpses of the future.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin terutama memakai sihir untuk melihat sekilas masa depan.",
        "en": "Odin mainly used magic to catch glimpses of the future."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c33",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Because of this cosying up to her, he is allowed three sips, but he simply chugs and drains the containers, shape-shifts into an eagle and legs it (well, wings it) out of there.",
      "locator": "Myths Involving Odin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kisah mead puisi, Odin diizinkan tiga tegukan tetapi menghabiskan seluruh wadahnya, lalu berubah wujud menjadi elang dan kabur.",
        "en": "In the story of the mead of poetry, Odin is allowed three sips but drains the containers, shape-shifts into an eagle and flees."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c34",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "resides in Asgard (the stronghold and home of the gods)",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Odin tinggal di Asgard, benteng dan kediaman para dewa.",
        "en": "Odin resides in Asgard, the stronghold and home of the gods."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c35",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Already in the Bronze Age do Swedish rock carvings depict figures of a god holding a spear, who with some imagination might be linked to Odin, and by at least 500 CE he clearly shows up on a range of ornaments alongside birds and warriors.",
      "locator": "Cult of Odin",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ukiran batu Swedia dari Zaman Perunggu menampilkan sosok dewa bertombak yang mungkin dikaitkan dengan Odin, dan paling lambat sekitar 500 M ia jelas muncul pada berbagai hiasan bersama burung dan prajurit.",
        "en": "Bronze Age Swedish rock carvings show figures of a god holding a spear who might be linked to Odin, and by at least 500 CE he clearly appears on ornaments alongside birds and warriors."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c36",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Odin's main group of worshippers mostly consisted of the elite - kings, chieftains & poets - but also included warriors.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para pemuja utama Odin sebagian besar adalah kalangan elite, yaitu raja, kepala suku, dan penyair, serta juga prajurit.",
        "en": "Odin's main group of worshippers mostly consisted of the elite, namely kings, chieftains and poets, but also included warriors."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c37",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "Skaldic poetry (Viking Age, pre-Christian poetry mainly heard at courts by kings and their retinues), for instance, names Baldr, Thor, and Vali as Odin's sons, whereas later on the 13th-century CE Icelandic author Snorri Sturluson also adds Heimdall, Týr, Bragi, Vidarr, and Hodr to the list.",
      "locator": "Functions",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Puisi skaldik menyebut Baldr, Thor, dan Vali sebagai putra Odin, sedangkan Snorri Sturluson pada abad ke-13 M menambahkan Heimdall, Týr, Bragi, Vidarr, dan Hodr.",
        "en": "Skaldic poetry names Baldr, Thor and Vali as Odin's sons, whereas the 13th-century CE author Snorri Sturluson adds Heimdall, Týr, Bragi, Vidarr and Hodr."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c38",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "in the Vafþrúðnismál Odin initiates a battle of wits with the giant Vafþrúðnir and beats him, while in the Grímnismál a disguised Odin is tortured and ends up revealing his extensive mythical knowledge to King Geirroðr.",
      "locator": "Myths Involving Odin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Vafþrúðnismál Odin memulai adu kecerdasan dengan raksasa Vafþrúðnir dan mengalahkannya; dalam Grímnismál Odin yang menyamar disiksa dan akhirnya membuka pengetahuannya kepada Raja Geirroðr.",
        "en": "In Vafþrúðnismál Odin starts a battle of wits with the giant Vafþrúðnir and beats him; in Grímnismál a disguised Odin is tortured and ends up revealing his mythical knowledge to King Geirroðr."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "odin-c39",
      "source_id": "odin-s1",
      "quote": "The modern English weekday name Wednesday traces its origins to \"day of Woden\", wōdnesdæg in Old English, and is still seen in the Dutch name woensdag.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama hari Wednesday dalam bahasa Inggris modern berasal dari \"hari Woden\" (wōdnesdæg dalam bahasa Inggris Kuno), dan masih terlihat pada nama Belanda woensdag.",
        "en": "The modern English weekday name Wednesday traces its origin to \"day of Woden\" (wōdnesdæg in Old English), still seen in the Dutch name woensdag."
      }
    }
  ],

    {
      "id": "Ra (juga ditulis Re) adalah dewa matahari Mesir kuno dan termasuk dewa tertua dalam jajaran dewa Mesir. Namanya berarti matahari dalam bahasa Mesir.       "en": "Ra (also written Re) is the sun god of ancient Egypt and one of the oldest deities in the Egyptian pantheon. His name is the Egyptian word for sun. He was later merged with other gods, such as Ra-Horakhty (the morning sun), Amun (the noonday sun), and Atum (the evening sun). Ra is almost always shown as the falcon-headed Ra-Horakhty with the solar disc above his head, and sometimes as a scarab beetle.",

Di sana, pada malam hari, bahteranya menjadi Kapal Sejuta Jiwa yang menjemput orang mati yang telah dibenarkan menuju Field of Reeds, dan Ra melebur dengan Osiris sebagai Ra-Osiris.       "en": "According to the sources, Ra ruled over the sky, the Earth, and the Duat (underworld).       "en": "In the best-known creation story, Ra the Self-Created stands on the primordial ben-ben mound that rose from swirling water, creates Hu and Sia, and then gives birth to Shu and Tefnut.         "id": "Tugas Ra sebagai dewa matahari adalah membawa matahari melintasi langit dengan bahtera suryanya untuk menerangi hari.",
        "en": "Ra's duty as the sun god was to carry the sun across the sky on his solar barque to light the day."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c37",
        "ra-q1252904-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "ability_id": "magic",
      "name": {
        "id": "Menciptakan dan memanfaatkan heka",
        "en": "Creating and harnessing heka"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Sebagai dewa pertama, Ra menciptakan heka, sihir ilahi, dan memanfaatkannya, sehingga lahirlah Heka yang kemudian memelihara dan mengendalikannya.",
        "en": "As the first god, Ra created heka, divine magic, and harnessed it, resulting in the birth of Heka, who then maintained and controlled it."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c24"
      ]
    },
    {
      "ability_id": "regeneration",
      "name": {
        "id": "Memperbarui diri di dunia bawah",
        "en": "Regenerating himself in the underworld"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Dalam pelayarannya di Dunia Bawah, Ra menerangi orang mati, menghancurkan musuh ciptaan, dan memperbarui dirinya dalam persatuan dengan Osiris.",
        "en": "During his voyage through the Underworld, Ra illuminates the dead, destroys the enemies of creation, and regenerates himself in union with Osiris."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c25"
      ]
    },
    {
      "ability_id": null,
      "name": {
        "id": "Mengarahkan musim",
        "en": "Directing the seasons"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Dalam kutipan Wilkinson, Ra dikatakan mengarahkan tiga musim dalam setahun Mesir, sehingga memengaruhi banjir Sungai Nil serta musim tanam dan panen.",
        "en": "In a passage by Wilkinson, Ra was said to direct the three seasons of the Egyptian year, influencing the Nile inundation and the growing and harvest seasons."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c20"
      ]
    },
    {
      "ability_id": null,
      "name": {
        "id": "Mengantar orang mati",
        "en": "Conveying the dead"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Dalam Teks Piramida Ra menghimpun jiwa raja dan membawanya ke Field of Reeds dengan bahtera emasnya; pada malam hari bahteranya menjemput orang mati yang telah dibenarkan.",
        "en": "In the Pyramid Texts Ra gathers the soul of the king and takes him to the Field of Reeds in his golden barge; at night his barque picks up the justified dead."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c40",
        "ra-q1252904-c17"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "weaknesses": [
    {
      "name": {
        "id": "Serangan Apophis",
        "en": "Apophis's attacks"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Setiap malam ular raksasa Apophis menyerang Ra di dunia bawah dan berusaha mencegah matahari terbit, tetapi selalu dikalahkan.",
        "en": "Every night the giant serpent Apophis attacks Ra in the underworld, trying to prevent the sun from rising, but is always defeated."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c04",
        "ra-q1252904-c19"
      ]
    },
    {
      "name": {
        "id": "Usia tua",
        "en": "Old age"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Dalam mitos, Ra menjadi terlalu tua dan lelah untuk memerintah di bumi dan pergi ke langit.",
        "en": "In myth, Ra became too old and weary to reign on Earth and went to the skies."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c36"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "variants": [
    {
      "name": "Ra-Horakhty",
      "tradition": {
        "id": "Mesir kuno",
        "en": "Ancient Egypt"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Wujud Ra yang dilebur dengan Horus, digambarkan sebagai lelaki berkepala elang dengan cakram matahari; ia dikaitkan dengan matahari pagi. Saat fajar Ra juga dapat berbentuk elang Hor-akhty.",
        "en": "Ra merged with Horus, depicted as a falcon-headed male with the solar disc; he is linked with the morning sun. At dawn Ra can also take the form of the falcon Hor-akhty."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c09",
        "ra-q1252904-c12",
        "ra-q1252904-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "name": "Ra-Khepri",
      "tradition": {
        "id": "Mesir kuno",
        "en": "Ancient Egypt"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Ra dapat ditampilkan sebagai kumbang scarab, kheper, \"yang menjadi ada\".",
        "en": "Ra could be shown as a scarab beetle, kheper, \"the one who comes into being\"."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c12",
        "ra-q1252904-c26"
      ]
    },
    {
      "name": "Amon-Ra / Amun-Ra",
      "tradition": {
        "id": "Mesir kuno",
        "en": "Ancient Egypt"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Peleburan Ra dengan dewa-dewa besar lain, misalnya Amon-Ra, Khnum-Ra, dan Sobek-Ra.",
        "en": "The synthesis of Ra with other great gods, for example Amon-Ra, Khnum-Ra, and Sobek-Ra."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c32"
      ]
    },
    {
      "name": "Ra-Osiris",
      "tradition": {
        "id": "Mesir kuno, dunia bawah",
        "en": "Ancient Egypt, the underworld"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Pada malam hari Ra melebur dengan Osiris; Osiris dipandang sebagai \"jasad\" dan Ra sebagai \"jiwa\" dewa tunggal itu.",
        "en": "At night Ra merges with Osiris; Osiris is seen as the \"corpse\" and Ra as the \"soul\" of the single deity."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c18"
      ]
    },
    {
      "name": "Banteng Mnevis",
      "tradition": {
        "id": "Heliopolis",
        "en": "Heliopolis"
      },
      "description": {
        "id": "Di Heliopolis Ra dipuja dalam rupa banteng Mnevis, perwujudan hidup Ra.",
        "en": "At Heliopolis Ra was worshiped in the form of the Mnevis bull, the living embodiment of Ra."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c44"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "stories": [
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Penciptaan dunia",
        "en": "The creation of the world"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Pencipta-Diri",
        "en": "The Self-Created creator"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Pada awal waktu hanya ada air bergolak sampai gundukan purba ben-ben muncul dan Ra sang Pencipta-Diri berdiri di atasnya. Ia menciptakan Hu dan Sia, lalu melahirkan Shu dan Tefnut yang pergi menata dunia.",
        "en": "At the beginning of time there was only swirling water until the primordial ben-ben mound rose and Ra the Self-Created stood upon it. He created Hu and Sia, then gave birth to Shu and Tefnut, who left to establish the world."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c13",
        "ra-q1252904-c14"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Kitab Sapi Langit",
        "en": "The Book of the Heavenly Cow"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Dewa yang menua dan meninggalkan bumi",
        "en": "The aging god who leaves the earth"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Ra memanggil Mata Ra, yang dalam kisah ini adalah Hathor, untuk menghancurkan manusia; ia membunuh ribuan orang sebelum Ra menyesal dan memintanya berhenti. Karena lelah memerintah manusia, Ra meminta Nut membawanya ke langit, dan Nut menjelma sapi langit.",
        "en": "Ra summons the Eye of Ra, who in this story is Hathor, to destroy humans; she kills thousands before Ra repents and begs her to stop. Tired of ruling humans, Ra asks Nut to carry him into the heavens, and Nut becomes a celestial cow."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c16",
        "ra-q1252904-c41",
        "ra-q1252904-c15"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Pelayaran malam melalui dunia bawah",
        "en": "The night voyage through the underworld"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Pemimpin bahtera yang menghadapi Apophis",
        "en": "Leader of the barque who faces Apophis"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Pada malam hari Ra berlayar di dunia bawah, menerangi orang mati dan memperbarui dirinya bersama Osiris. Bahteranya menjemput orang mati yang telah dibenarkan menuju Field of Reeds, dan Apophis yang menyerang setiap malam selalu dikalahkan.",
        "en": "At night Ra sails the underworld, illuminating the dead and regenerating himself with Osiris. His barque picks up the justified dead and brings them to the Field of Reeds, and Apophis, who attacks every night, is always defeated."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c25",
        "ra-q1252904-c17",
        "ra-q1252904-c19"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Manusia memberontak",
        "en": "Humanity rebels"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Dewa yang memutuskan pemusnahan",
        "en": "The god who decides on extermination"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Ketika Ra yang menua menyempurnakan ciptaannya, manusia memberontak. Ra memutuskan memusnahkan mereka dan meminta bantuan matanya.",
        "en": "While the aging Ra was fine-tuning his creations, humanity rebelled. Ra decided on extermination and asked his eye for help."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "ra-q1252904-c27"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "description": {

  "modern_depictions": [],
  "tradition_vs_modern": {
    "traditional": {
      "id": "Dalam tradisi Mesir kuno, Ra adalah dewa matahari yang melintasi langit dengan bahtera suryanya dan turun ke dunia bawah saat matahari terbenam.",
      "en": "In ancient Egyptian tradition Ra is the sun god who crosses the sky in his solar barque and descends into the underworld at sunset."
    },
    "modern": {
      "id": "Menurut sumber ini, gema Ra masih terdengar dalam cerita rakyat Mesir masa kini dan nama Ra muncul dalam film Hollywood dan gim video.",
      "en": "According to this source, echoes of Ra are still heard in folklore in present-day Egypt, and the name Ra appears in Hollywood films and video games."
    },
    "claim_ids": [
      "ra-q1252904-c03",
      "ra-q1252904-c49",
      "ra-q1252904-c08"
    ]
  },
  "learning_questions": [
    {
      "id": "Mengapa Ra digambarkan dalam beberapa wujud berbeda pada pagi, siang, dan petang?",
      "en": "Why is Ra shown in different forms at morning, noon, and evening?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Apa arti pertempuran nyata setiap malam antara Ra dan Apophis bagi orang Mesir kuno?",
      "en": "What did the nightly battle between Ra and Apophis mean for the ancient Egyptians?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Bagaimana dewa-dewa lain dapat \"melebur\" dengan Ra tanpa kehilangan identitasnya?",
      "en": "How could other gods merge with Ra without losing their own identity?"
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c01",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra (also given as Re) is the sun god of ancient Egypt.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra adalah dewa matahari Mesir kuno.",
        "en": "Ra is the ancient Egyptian sun god."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c02",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra is the Egyptian word for 'sun'.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Ra berarti matahari dalam bahasa Mesir.",
        "en": "Ra is the Egyptian word for sun."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c03",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "As a solar deity, Ra embodied the power of the sun but was also thought to be the sun itself, envisioned as the great god riding in his barge across the heavens throughout the day and descending into the underworld at sunset.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra melintasi langit dengan bahtera pada siang hari dan turun ke dunia bawah saat matahari terbenam.",
        "en": "Ra crosses the sky in a barque by day and descends to the underworld at sunset."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c04",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "As he made his way through the darkness beneath the earth, he was attacked nightly by the giant serpent Apophis (also known as Apep) who tried to prevent the sun from rising and so destroy all life on earth.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ular Apophis menyerang Ra di dunia bawah setiap malam.",
        "en": "The serpent Apophis attacks Ra in the underworld each night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c05",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "An island emerged from the water to support this divinity, who manifested itself in the form of Ra, the sun god of Egypt.",
      "locator": "Creation story",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kisah penciptaan, dewa pencipta menampakkan diri sebagai Ra di pulau yang muncul dari air.",
        "en": "In the creation story, the deity manifests as Ra on an island emerging from water."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c06",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "When Ra appeared at dawn in the Eastern horizon, he took the form of a falcon, known as Hor-akhty, or Horus of the Horizon, the falcon who flies high in the sky (Horus = one who is high up.)",
      "locator": "Ra's forms",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saat fajar Ra dapat berbentuk elang Hor-akhty.",
        "en": "At dawn Ra can appear as the falcon Hor-akhty."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c07",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "At sunset he became Atum, an old man who had completed his life cycle and was ready to disappear to be regenerated for a new day.",
      "locator": "Ra's forms",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saat matahari terbenam Ra menjadi Atum.",
        "en": "At sunset Ra becomes Atum."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c08",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "The word Ra also appears in everything from Hollywood films to video games.",
      "locator": "Modern reception",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Ra juga muncul dalam film Hollywood dan gim video.",
        "en": "Ra's name also appears in Hollywood films and video games."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c09",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra (also given as Re) is the sun god of ancient Egypt. He is one of the oldest deities in the Egyptian pantheon and was later merged with others such as Horus, becoming Ra-Horakhty (the morning sun), Amun (as noonday sun), and Atum (the evening sun) associated with primal life-giving energy.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra termasuk dewa tertua dalam jajaran dewa Mesir dan kemudian dilebur dengan dewa lain: Ra-Horakhty (matahari pagi), Amun (matahari siang), dan Atum (matahari petang).",
        "en": "Ra is one of the oldest deities in the Egyptian pantheon and was later merged with others: Ra-Horakhty (the morning sun), Amun (the noonday sun), and Atum (the evening sun)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c10",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra is first mentioned in the Pyramid Texts (c. 2400-2300 BCE), the oldest religious works in the world, which were inscribed on the sarcophagi and walls of tombs at Saqqara.",
      "locator": "Early Depiction, Origin, & Worship",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra pertama kali disebut dalam Teks Piramida (sekitar 2400-2300 SM) yang terpahat pada sarkofagus dan dinding makam di Saqqara.",
        "en": "Ra is first mentioned in the Pyramid Texts (c. 2400-2300 BCE), inscribed on sarcophagi and tomb walls at Saqqara."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c11",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Worship of Ra was already well established at the time these texts - which are thought to derive from a much earlier oral tradition - were inscribed. His cult center was at the city of Iunu (better known as Heliopolis, the Greek name, which means \"city of the sun god\").",
      "locator": "Early Depiction, Origin, & Worship",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pusat pemujaan Ra berada di kota Iunu, yang lebih dikenal dengan nama Yunaninya, Heliopolis, \"kota dewa matahari\".",
        "en": "Ra's cult center was the city of Iunu, better known by its Greek name Heliopolis, \"city of the sun god\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c12",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "He is almost always depicted as the falcon-headed male Ra-Horakhty with the solar disc above his head although sometimes also shown as the scarab beetle below the solar disc (in this form he is known as Ra-Khepri.",
      "locator": "Early Depiction, Origin, & Worship",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra hampir selalu digambarkan sebagai Ra-Horakhty berkepala elang dengan cakram matahari di atas kepalanya, kadang juga sebagai kumbang scarab di bawah cakram matahari.",
        "en": "Ra is almost always depicted as the falcon-headed Ra-Horakhty with the solar disc above his head, sometimes also as the scarab beetle below the solar disc."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c13",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "In the best-known version of the story, there was nothing but swirling waters at the beginning of time when the primordial mound (the ben-ben) rose as the first dry land and Ra the Self-Created stood upon it.",
      "locator": "Ra as Creator",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam versi yang paling dikenal, pada awal waktu hanya ada air bergolak, lalu gundukan purba (ben-ben) muncul sebagai daratan pertama dan Ra sang Pencipta-Diri berdiri di atasnya.",
        "en": "In the best-known version, at the beginning of time there was only swirling water until the primordial mound (the ben-ben) rose as the first dry land and Ra the Self-Created stood upon it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c14",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra the Self-Created stood upon it. He cut his penis open and, from the drops of blood, created Hu (authority) and Sia (mind), and then became aware of his solitude and mated with his own shadow to give birth to Shu (god of air) and Tefnut (goddess of moisture) who left to establish the world.",
      "locator": "Ra as Creator",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kisah penciptaan itu Ra menciptakan Hu (otoritas) dan Sia (pikiran), lalu melahirkan Shu (dewa udara) dan Tefnut (dewi kelembapan), yang pergi untuk menata dunia.",
        "en": "In that creation story Ra creates Hu (authority) and Sia (mind), then gives birth to Shu (god of air) and Tefnut (goddess of moisture), who left to establish the world."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c15",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra is tired of ruling over humans, however, and asks the goddess Nut to carry him into the heavens. Nut turns herself into a celestial cow and takes Ra skyward on her back.",
      "locator": "Ra in the Heavens",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Kitab Sapi Langit, Ra yang lelah memerintah manusia meminta dewi Nut membawanya ke langit, dan Nut menjelma sapi langit yang membawanya naik di punggungnya.",
        "en": "In the Book of the Heavenly Cow, a Ra tired of ruling humans asks the goddess Nut to carry him into the heavens, and Nut becomes a celestial cow and takes him skyward on her back."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c16",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra summons The Eye of Ra, usually personified as a goddess, which is a powerful force that alternately does Ra's bidding or breaks free of his control to wreak havoc.",
      "locator": "Ra in the Heavens",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra memanggil Mata Ra, yang biasanya dipersonifikasikan sebagai dewi, kekuatan dahsyat yang bergantian menuruti perintah Ra atau lepas dari kendalinya dan mengamuk.",
        "en": "Ra summons the Eye of Ra, usually personified as a goddess, a powerful force that alternately does Ra's bidding or breaks free of his control to wreak havoc."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c17",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "The sun barge now transformed into the evening barge known as the Ship of a Million Souls which picked up the newly arrived and justified dead to bring them to the paradise of the Field of Reeds.",
      "locator": "Ra in the Netherworld",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada malam hari bahtera matahari berubah menjadi bahtera malam, Kapal Sejuta Jiwa, yang menjemput orang mati yang telah dibenarkan menuju surga Field of Reeds.",
        "en": "At night the sun barge becomes the evening barge, the Ship of a Million Souls, which picks up the newly arrived and justified dead and brings them to the paradise of the Field of Reeds."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c18",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra at this time becomes merged with Osiris, the judge of the dead, and Osiris is seen as the \"corpse\" and Ra as the \"soul\" of the single deity Ra-Osiris.",
      "locator": "Ra in the Netherworld",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada saat itu Ra melebur dengan Osiris, hakim orang mati; Osiris dipandang sebagai \"jasad\" dan Ra sebagai \"jiwa\" dewa tunggal Ra-Osiris.",
        "en": "At that time Ra merges with Osiris, the judge of the dead; Osiris is seen as the \"corpse\" and Ra as the \"soul\" of the single deity Ra-Osiris."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c19",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "The gods onboard fight the serpent off with the help of the justified dead while, on earth, the living encourage the defenders through ritual ceremonies, channeling positive energies to strengthen those on board. Every night Apophis attacks, and every night he is defeated.",
      "locator": "Ra in the Netherworld",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para dewa di atas bahtera, dibantu orang mati yang dibenarkan, menangkis ular itu; Apophis menyerang setiap malam dan setiap malam ia dikalahkan.",
        "en": "The gods aboard, helped by the justified dead, fight the serpent off; Apophis attacks every night and every night he is defeated."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c20",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "A specific and important example of Ra's influence on the earth is seen in that the god was said to direct the three seasons of the Egyptian year - thus influencing the annual inundation of the Nile and the subsequent growing and harvest seasons.",
      "locator": "Ra on the Earth",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kutipan Wilkinson dalam sumber ini, Ra dikatakan mengarahkan tiga musim dalam setahun Mesir, sehingga memengaruhi banjir tahunan Sungai Nil serta musim tanam dan panen.",
        "en": "In a passage by Wilkinson quoted in this source, the god was said to direct the three seasons of the Egyptian year, influencing the annual Nile inundation and the growing and harvest seasons."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c21",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "The founder of the 5th Dynasty of the Old Kingdom, the king Userkaf (r. late 26th century BCE), epitomized this ideal in elevating the Cult of Ra to the position of almost a state religion and encouraging his subjects to adhere to Ra's values.",
      "locator": "Ra as King & Father of the King",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Raja Userkaf, pendiri Dinasti ke-5 Kerajaan Lama, mengangkat pemujaan Ra hampir menjadi agama negara.",
        "en": "King Userkaf, founder of the 5th Dynasty of the Old Kingdom, elevated the Cult of Ra to almost a state religion."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c22",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "According to scholar Richard H. Wilkinson, Ra is \"arguably Egypt's most important deity\" not only because of his association with the life-giving sun but also through his influence on the development of later gods",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut sarjana Richard H. Wilkinson, Ra \"boleh dikatakan dewa terpenting Mesir\", karena kaitannya dengan matahari pemberi kehidupan dan pengaruhnya pada perkembangan dewa-dewa kemudian.",
        "en": "According to scholar Richard H. Wilkinson, Ra is \"arguably Egypt's most important deity\", because of his link to the life-giving sun and his influence on the development of later gods."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c23",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Amun, who would become so popular that his cult was almost monotheistic in devotion and the most powerful in ancient Egypt, developed from Ra and shares much of his mythology.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amun, yang kelak sangat populer, berkembang dari Ra dan berbagi banyak mitologinya.",
        "en": "Amun, who would become hugely popular, developed from Ra and shares much of his mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c24",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Ra, as the first god, created heka and harnessed it, resulting in the birth of Heka who then maintained and controlled the divine magic afterwards.",
      "locator": "Early Depiction, Origin, & Worship",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai dewa pertama, Ra menciptakan heka (sihir ilahi) dan memanfaatkannya, sehingga lahirlah Heka yang kemudian memelihara dan mengendalikannya.",
        "en": "As the first god, Ra created heka (divine magic) and harnessed it, resulting in the birth of Heka, who then maintained and controlled it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c25",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "Reaching the Western horizon, Ra then left the earth in darkness for 12 hours of night while he sailed the Underworld, illuminating the dead, destroying the enemies of creation, and regenerating himself in a union with Osiris, the god of resurrection.",
      "locator": "Ra's journey",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah mencapai cakrawala barat, Ra berlayar di Dunia Bawah selama 12 jam malam, menerangi orang mati, menghancurkan musuh ciptaan, dan memperbarui dirinya dalam persatuan dengan Osiris.",
        "en": "Reaching the Western horizon, Ra sails the Underworld for 12 hours of night, illuminating the dead, destroying the enemies of creation, and regenerating himself in union with Osiris."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c26",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "But Ra had other forms. He also could be represented as a scarab called kheper (the one who comes into being)",
      "locator": "Ra's forms",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra juga dapat digambarkan sebagai kumbang scarab bernama kheper, \"yang menjadi ada\".",
        "en": "Ra could also be represented as a scarab called kheper, \"the one who comes into being\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c27",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "While the aging Ra was fine-tuning his creations, humanity rebelled against him. The god decided on extermination, asking his tear-giving eye again for help.",
      "locator": "Ra's journey",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketika Ra yang menua menyempurnakan ciptaannya, umat manusia memberontak; ia memutuskan memusnahkan mereka dan meminta bantuan matanya.",
        "en": "While the aging Ra was fine-tuning his creations, humanity rebelled; he decided on extermination and asked his eye for help."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c28",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "during the reign of the so-called heretic king Akhenaten (1353-1336 BCE), when he closed down and outlawed every other cult, he allowed the cult of Ra to remain and worship to continue.",
      "locator": "Conclusion",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada masa Raja Akhenaten (1353-1336 SM), yang menutup dan melarang semua kultus lain, pemujaan Ra tetap dibiarkan berlanjut.",
        "en": "During the reign of Akhenaten (1353-1336 BCE), who closed down and outlawed every other cult, the cult of Ra was allowed to continue."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c29",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "And by the way, humankind happened out of the tears of his eyes.",
      "locator": "Creation story",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut sumber ini, umat manusia lahir dari air mata mata Ra.",
        "en": "According to this source, humankind came into being from the tears of Ra's eyes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c30",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "In the Old Kingdom (2800 BCE), when Egypt established its institutions and expressed its royal ideology, the divinized king of Egypt was considered the son of the Sun God.",
      "locator": "Ra and kingship",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Kerajaan Lama (2800 SM) raja Mesir yang diilahikan dianggap putra Dewa Matahari.",
        "en": "In the Old Kingdom (2800 BCE) the divinized king of Egypt was considered the son of the Sun God."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c31",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "It would take yet more centuries and Roman conquest before the cult of Ra declined.",
      "locator": "Ra and kingship",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemujaan Ra baru surut setelah berabad-abad lagi dan penaklukan Romawi.",
        "en": "The cult of Ra declined only after yet more centuries and the Roman conquest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c32",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s2",
      "quote": "most of Egypt's great gods synthesized with Ra as Amon-Ra, Khnum-Ra, Sobek-Ra, and so forth.",
      "locator": "Ra and kingship",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian besar dewa besar Mesir menyatu dengan Ra, misalnya sebagai Amon-Ra, Khnum-Ra, dan Sobek-Ra.",
        "en": "Most of Egypt's great gods synthesized with Ra, as Amon-Ra, Khnum-Ra, Sobek-Ra, and so forth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c33",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s3",
      "quote": "All forms of life were believed to have been created by Ra. In some accounts, humans were created from Ra's tears and sweat, hence the Egyptians called themselves the \"Cattle of Ra\".",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Semua bentuk kehidupan dipercaya diciptakan oleh Ra; dalam sebagian kisah manusia tercipta dari air mata dan keringat Ra, sehingga orang Mesir menyebut diri \"Ternak Ra\".",
        "en": "All forms of life were believed to have been created by Ra; in some accounts humans were created from Ra's tears and sweat, hence the Egyptians called themselves the \"Cattle of Ra\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c34",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s3",
      "quote": "Ra ruled in all parts of the created world: the sky, the Earth, and the Duat (underworld).",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra berkuasa di seluruh bagian dunia ciptaan: langit, bumi, dan Duat (dunia bawah).",
        "en": "Ra ruled in all parts of the created world: the sky, the Earth, and the Duat (underworld)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c35",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s3",
      "quote": "He was believed to have ruled as the second pharaoh of Ancient Egypt, succeeding Ptah. He was the god of the Sun, order, kings and the sky.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ra dipercaya memerintah sebagai firaun kedua Mesir kuno, menggantikan Ptah, dan ia adalah dewa matahari, ketertiban, raja, dan langit.",
        "en": "Ra was believed to have ruled as the second pharaoh of Ancient Egypt, succeeding Ptah, and was the god of the Sun, order, kings and the sky."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c36",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s3",
      "quote": "According to Egyptian myth, when Ra became too old and weary to reign on Earth, he relinquished and went to the skies.",
      "locator": "Religious roles",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut mitos Mesir, ketika Ra terlalu tua dan lelah untuk memerintah di bumi, ia melepaskan takhta dan pergi ke langit.",
        "en": "According to Egyptian myth, when Ra became too old and weary to reign on Earth, he relinquished his rule and went to the skies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c37",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s3",
      "quote": "As the Sun god, his duty was to carry the Sun across the sky on his solar barque to light the day.",
      "locator": "Religious roles",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai dewa matahari, tugas Ra membawa matahari melintasi langit dengan bahtera suryanya untuk menerangi hari.",
        "en": "As the Sun god, Ra's duty was to carry the Sun across the sky on his solar barque to light the day."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c38",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Worship of Ra was already established by the time of the Old Kingdom of Egypt (c. 2613-2181 BCE) and continued for almost 2,000 years until, like the other Egyptian gods, he was eclipsed by Christianity.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemujaan Ra sudah mapan pada Kerajaan Lama Mesir (sekitar 2613-2181 SM) dan berlanjut hampir 2.000 tahun hingga ia dikalahkan Kekristenan.",
        "en": "Worship of Ra was already established by the Old Kingdom (c. 2613-2181 BCE) and continued for almost 2,000 years until he was eclipsed by Christianity."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c39",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Userkaf and his successors referred to themselves as \"sons of Ra\" and were understood as literally being the children of the god.",
      "locator": "Ra as King & Father of the King",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Userkaf dan penerusnya menyebut diri \"putra Ra\" dan dipahami benar-benar sebagai anak sang dewa.",
        "en": "Userkaf and his successors called themselves \"sons of Ra\" and were understood as literally being the god's children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c40",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "In these, Ra gathers the soul of the king to himself and takes him to the paradise of the Field of Reeds in his golden barge.",
      "locator": "Early Depiction, Origin, & Worship",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Teks Piramida, Ra menghimpun jiwa raja dan membawanya ke surga Field of Reeds dengan bahtera emasnya.",
        "en": "In the Pyramid Texts, Ra gathers the soul of the king to himself and takes him to the paradise of the Field of Reeds in his golden barge."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c41",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Hathor is the Eye of Ra here and is released to destroy humanity. She kills thousands before Ra realizes what he has done and repents, begging her to stop.",
      "locator": "Ra in the Heavens",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Kitab Sapi Langit, Hathor sebagai Mata Ra dilepaskan untuk menghancurkan manusia dan membunuh ribuan orang sebelum Ra menyesal dan memintanya berhenti.",
        "en": "In the Book of the Heavenly Cow, Hathor as the Eye of Ra is released to destroy humanity and kills thousands before Ra repents and begs her to stop."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ra-q1252904-c42",
      "source_id": "ra-q1252904-s1",
      "quote": "Userkaf built the Nekhenre - the Sun Temple of Ra - near Abusir and established a tradition venerating Ra which continued throughout the dynasty.",
      "locator": "Ra as King & Father of the King",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Userkaf membangun Nekhenre, Kuil Matahari Ra, di dekat Abusir dan memulai tradisi memuliakan Ra.",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Hestia adalah dewi perawan perapian dan rumah dalam mitologi Yunani, putri sulung Cronus dan Rhea, yang menerima bagian pertama dari setiap kurban.",
    "en": "Hestia is the virgin Greek goddess of the hearth and home, eldest daughter of Cronus and Rhea, who received the first share of every sacrifice.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "hestia-c01",
      "hestia-c02",
      "hestia-c12"

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Hestia adalah dewi perawan perapian, rumah, dan keramahtamahan dalam kepercayaan Yunani. Dalam mitos ia putri sulung Cronus dan Rhea, ditelan ayahnya saat lahir lalu dimuntahkan kembali oleh Zeus. Sebagai pelindung keluarga dan komunitas politik, persembahan dibuat bagi Hestia di perapian setiap rumah dan di perapian umum kota. Bangsa Romawi mengenalnya sebagai Vesta.",
      "en": "Hestia is the virgin Greek goddess of the hearth, home, and hospitality. In myth she is the eldest daughter of Cronus and Rhea, swallowed by her father at birth and later disgorged by Zeus. As protector of the family and political community, offerings were made to Hestia at the hearth in every home and at a city's public hearth. The Romans knew her as Vesta.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "hestia-c01",
        "hestia-c02",
        "hestia-c24",
        "hestia-c08",
        "hestia-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Hestia menerima kurban lebih dulu daripada dewa lain, juga persembahan anggur pertama dan terakhir pada setiap pesta, dan biasanya disebut pertama dalam doa dan sumpah. Menurut mitos, Zeus memberinya kehormatan itu ketika ia bersumpah tetap perawan, meskipun Apollo, Poseidon, dan Priapus mendekatinya. Perapian melambangkan jantung dan jiwa rumah tangga, dan kaitannya dengan perapian menghubungkan Hestia dengan keramahtamahan serta perlindungan tamu. Di kota, ia terutama dikaitkan dengan prytaneion dan bouleuterion, pusat simbolis pemerintahan setempat.",
      "en": "Hestia received sacrifices before the other gods, as well as the first and last libations of wine at any feast, and was usually mentioned first in prayers and oaths. According to myth, Zeus gave her this honour when she swore to remain a virgin, despite the attentions of Apollo, Poseidon, and Priapus. The hearth symbolised the heart and soul of the household, and her link with the hearth tied Hestia to hospitality and the protection of guests. In the city her special place was the prytaneion and bouleuterion, the symbolic seat of local government.",
      "claim_ids": [

        "hestia-c16"

    {
      "id": "Hestia tidak punya mitologi yang menonjol: ia digambarkan sebagai dewi senior yang menjauh dari urusan para dewa, dan ia satu-satunya yang tidak ikut serangan gagal para dewa Olimpus terhadap Zeus. Dalam sebagian daftar ia termasuk 12 dewa Olimpus, tetapi lebih umum tempatnya diisi Dionysos; dalam sebagian mitos ia sendiri menyerahkan tempat itu kepada Dionysos. Dalam seni ia biasanya digambarkan sebagai perempuan muda berkerudung dan berpakaian sederhana, sulit dibedakan dari dewi lain.",
      "en": "Hestia has no prominent mythology: she is presented as a senior goddess aloof from the affairs of the other gods, and alone among the goddesses she stayed out of the Olympians' unsuccessful assault on Zeus. Some lists count her among the 12 Olympians, though Dionysos more often fills that seat; some myths have her yield the seat to him herself. In art she is usually shown as a young woman, veiled and modestly dressed, and is hard to tell apart from other goddesses.",

        "hestia-c11",

        "hestia-c20"

    {
      "id": "Pemujaan umum Hestia sangat marak di Attica, dengan kultus penting di Piraeus, Eleusis, Halimos, dan Krokonidai. Imam dan imam perempuan yang khusus mengabdi kepadanya tampak lazim sejak masa Helenistik, dan pada masa Romawi, ketika ia dikenal sebagai Vesta, ia terus dipuja, misalnya di Ephesus. Setiap kali koloni baru didirikan, nyala api dari perapian umum Hestia di kota induk dibawa ke permukiman baru.",
      "en": "Her public cult flourished above all in Attica, with notable cults at Piraeus, Eleusis, Halimos, and Krokonidai. Priests and priestesses specifically dedicated to her seem to have been common from the Hellenistic period, and in the Roman period, when she was known as Vesta, she continued to be worshipped, for example at Ephesus. Whenever a new colony was founded, a flame from Hestia's public hearth in the mother city was carried to the new settlement.",

    "id": "Perapian dipandang sebagai pusat suci kehidupan rumah tangga dan lambang jantung serta jiwa rumah tangga; Hestia dianggap pemberi kebahagiaan dan berkat rumah tangga, dan sumpah serta suaka pemohon dikaitkan dengan perapian. Kurban bagi Hestia berupa buah sulung, air, minyak, anggur, dan sapi berumur satu tahun, dan api perapiannya yang padam tidak boleh dinyalakan kembali dengan api biasa.",

      "summary": {
        "id": "Cronus, yang takut ditumbangkan anaknya, menelan Hestia dan saudara-saudaranya. Zeus kemudian memaksa sang Titan memuntahkan mereka; karena Hestia ditelan pertama dan dimuntahkan terakhir, ia disebut sekaligus yang tertua dan termuda.",

      "summary": {

        "en": "When Apollon and Poseidon sought her hand, Hestia refused and asked Zeus to let her remain a virgin. Zeus agreed, gave her a place in the midst of the house and the honour of the first sacrifice, and she took her place at his hearth."
