      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Dalam sebagian mitos Hestia melepaskan tempatnya di Gunung Olimpus dan bertukar dengan Dionysos, karena lebih suka menjauh dari urusan para dewa dan yakin disambut hangat di kota manusia mana pun.",
        "en": "Some myths say Hestia stepped down from her seat on Mount Olympus in favour of Dionysos, choosing to stay out of divine affairs, confident that any human city would welcome her."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "hestia-c11",
        "hestia-c10"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Tidak ikut menyerang Zeus",
        "en": "Not joining the attack on Zeus"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Satu-satunya dewi yang tidak ikut",
        "en": "The only goddess who did not join"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Hestia adalah satu-satunya dewi yang tidak ikut para dewa Olimpus dalam serangan gagal mereka terhadap Zeus.",
        "en": "Hestia was the only goddess who did not join the Olympians in their failed attack on Zeus."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "hestia-c18"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Priapus dan Vesta dalam Fasti Ovidius",
        "en": "Priapus and Vesta in Ovid's Fasti"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Dewi yang diselamatkan dari gangguan",
        "en": "The goddess saved from an advance"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Dalam Fasti Ovidius, Vesta (Hestia) tertidur saat pesta para dewa, dan Priapus mendekatinya. Keledai Silenus meringkik, sang dewi terbangun ketakutan, dan para hadirin berlari menolongnya sementara Priapus melarikan diri.",
        "en": "In Ovid's Fasti, Vesta (Hestia) falls asleep during the gods' feast and Priapus approaches her. Silenus's donkey brays, the goddess wakes frightened, and the crowd rushes to her while Priapus flees."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "hestia-c39",
        "hestia-c40"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "learning_questions": [
    {
      "id": "Mengapa perapian bisa menjadi simbol pusat rumah tangga sekaligus pusat kota bagi orang Yunani kuno?",
      "en": "Why could the hearth be a symbol of both the household's and the city's centre for the ancient Greeks?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Apa arti kehormatan menerima kurban pertama bagi kedudukan Hestia di antara para dewa?",
      "en": "What does the honour of the first sacrifice mean for Hestia's standing among the gods?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Mengapa dewi yang hampir tak punya mitos bisa begitu sering disebut dalam doa dan sumpah?",
      "en": "Why might a goddess with almost no myths be named so often in prayers and oaths?"
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "hestia-c01",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "Hestia was the Greek virgin goddess of the hearth, home, and hospitality.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia adalah dewi perapian, rumah, dan keramahtamahan Yunani.",
        "en": "Hestia is the Greek goddess of the hearth, home, and hospitality."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c02",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "In Greek mythology, she is the eldest daughter of Cronus and Rhea.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia adalah putri sulung Cronus dan Rhea.",
        "en": "Hestia is the eldest daughter of Cronus and Rhea."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c03",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "To the Romans, the goddess was known as Vesta.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bangsa Romawi mengenalnya sebagai Vesta.",
        "en": "The Romans knew her as Vesta."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c04",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "Hestia never married and remained a virgin, despite the amorous attentions of Apollo, Poseidon, and Priapus, the fertility god.",
      "locator": "Family",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia tetap perawan dan tidak menikah meskipun didekati Apollo, Poseidon, dan Priapus.",
        "en": "Hestia remained unmarried despite the interest of Apollo, Poseidon, and Priapus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c05",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "The goddess was the personification of the hearth and so she received sacrifices in all the temples of the gods as each one had its own hearth.",
      "locator": "The Hearth in Greek Culture",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia dipandang sebagai personifikasi perapian dan menerima persembahan di kuil para dewa.",
        "en": "Hestia personified the hearth and received offerings in temples."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c06",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "Hestia was also the goddess of the sacrificial flame and received a share of every sacrifice to the gods.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia terkait api kurban dan mendapat bagian dari setiap persembahan kepada para dewa.",
        "en": "Hestia governed the sacrificial flame and received a share of each offering."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c07",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "Hestia was depicted in Athenian vase painting as a modestly veiled woman sometimes holding a flowered branch (perhaps a chaste-tree).",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lukisan vas Athena menggambarkan Hestia sebagai perempuan berkerudung, kadang memegang dahan berbunga.",
        "en": "Athenian vase paintings show Hestia veiled and sometimes holding a flowering branch."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c08",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "In her role as a protector of the family and political community, sacrifices and offerings were regularly made to Hestia at the hearth within each private home and at the town or city's public hearth.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai pelindung keluarga dan komunitas politik, persembahan secara rutin dibuat bagi Hestia di perapian setiap rumah dan di perapian umum kota.",
        "en": "As protector of the family and political community, offerings were regularly made to Hestia at the hearth in each private home and at the town or city's public hearth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c09",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "In the Greek myths, Hestia's parents were Cronus and Rhea and so her younger siblings were Zeus, Hera, Demeter, Poseidon, and Hades. Cronus, paranoid that one of his own children would overthrow his rule, swallowed them all.",
      "locator": "Family",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang tua Hestia adalah Cronus dan Rhea; adik-adiknya Zeus, Hera, Demeter, Poseidon, dan Hades. Cronus, yang takut ditumbangkan anaknya, menelan mereka semua.",
        "en": "Hestia's parents were Cronus and Rhea; her younger siblings were Zeus, Hera, Demeter, Poseidon, and Hades. Cronus, fearing overthrow by his children, swallowed them all."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c10",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "In some lists, Hestia is one of the 12 Olympian gods but more commonly her place is taken by Dionysos.",
      "locator": "Family",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sebagian daftar Hestia termasuk 12 dewa Olimpus, tetapi lebih umum tempatnya diisi Dionysos.",
        "en": "In some lists Hestia is one of the 12 Olympian gods, but more commonly her place is taken by Dionysos."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c11",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "In some myths the goddess voluntarily relinquishes her place amongst the gods on Mount Olympus, swapping with Dionysos because she prefers to withdraw from godly affairs and is sure of a fond welcome in any mortal city she chooses.",
      "locator": "Family",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sebagian mitos Hestia melepaskan tempatnya di Gunung Olimpus dan bertukar dengan Dionysos karena lebih suka menjauh dari urusan para dewa dan yakin disambut hangat di kota manusia mana pun.",
        "en": "In some myths Hestia voluntarily gives up her place on Mount Olympus, swapping with Dionysos because she prefers to withdraw from godly affairs and is sure of a warm welcome in any mortal city."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c12",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "By tradition, Hestia received any sacrifices before the other gods, even at such places as Olympia where Zeus was honoured. Hestia also received the first and last libations of wine offering at any feast and was usually mentioned first in any prayers and oaths.",
      "locator": "The Hearth in Greek Culture",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tradisi, Hestia menerima kurban lebih dulu daripada dewa lain, mendapat persembahan anggur pertama dan terakhir pada setiap pesta, dan biasanya disebut pertama dalam doa dan sumpah.",
        "en": "By tradition Hestia received sacrifices before the other gods, received the first and last libations of wine at any feast, and was usually mentioned first in prayers and oaths."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c13",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "In mythology, the honour of receiving the first sacrifice was given by Zeus when Hestia swore that she would always remain a virgin.",
      "locator": "The Hearth in Greek Culture",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi, kehormatan menerima kurban pertama diberikan Zeus ketika Hestia bersumpah akan selalu perawan.",
        "en": "In mythology Zeus gave Hestia the honour of the first sacrifice when she swore she would always remain a virgin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c14",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "Within a Greek house, the hearth was usually a portable brazier but it symbolised the heart and soul of the household and came to represent its centre.",
      "locator": "The Hearth in Greek Culture",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di rumah Yunani, perapian biasanya berupa tungku portabel, tetapi melambangkan jantung dan jiwa rumah tangga dan menjadi pusatnya.",
        "en": "In a Greek house the hearth was usually a portable brazier but symbolised the heart and soul of the household and came to represent its centre."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c15",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "The goddesses personification as the hearth also linked her to ideas of hospitality, the protection of guests, and the inventor of good housebuilding.",
      "locator": "The Hearth in Greek Culture",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Personifikasi Hestia sebagai perapian juga mengaitkannya dengan keramahtamahan, perlindungan tamu, dan penemu pembangunan rumah yang baik.",
        "en": "Hestia's personification as the hearth also linked her to hospitality, the protection of guests, and the inventor of good housebuilding."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c16",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "Besides individual homes, Hestia was particularly associated with the prytaneion and bouleuterion, the symbolic centre of a town or city where civic functions were held and the business of local government was carried out.",
      "locator": "The Hearth in Greek Culture",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Selain rumah pribadi, Hestia terutama dikaitkan dengan prytaneion dan bouleuterion, pusat simbolis kota tempat urusan pemerintahan setempat dijalankan.",
        "en": "Besides individual homes, Hestia was particularly associated with the prytaneion and bouleuterion, the symbolic centre of a town where local government business was carried out."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c17",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "Public worship of Hestia was especially prevalent in Attica with notable cults at Piraeus, Eleusis, Halimos, and Krokonidai.",
      "locator": "Cults to the Goddess",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemujaan umum Hestia terutama marak di Attica dengan kultus penting di Piraeus, Eleusis, Halimos, dan Krokonidai.",
        "en": "Public worship of Hestia was especially prevalent in Attica, with notable cults at Piraeus, Eleusis, Halimos, and Krokonidai."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c18",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "The Greek goddess was the only one to not join the Olympians in their failed attack on Zeus.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia adalah satu-satunya dewi yang tidak ikut para dewa Olimpus dalam serangan gagal mereka terhadap Zeus.",
        "en": "Hestia was the only goddess who did not join the Olympians in their failed attack on Zeus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c19",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "Hestia is usually presented in Greek art as a young female wearing a head covering & modest clothing.",
      "locator": "The Hearth in Greek Culture",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam seni Yunani, Hestia biasanya ditampilkan sebagai perempuan muda berpenutup kepala dan berpakaian sederhana.",
        "en": "In Greek art Hestia is usually presented as a young woman wearing a head covering and modest clothing."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c20",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "As Hestia has no particular mythology surrounding her, she does not appear very often in Greek art, and when she does, she can be difficult to distinguish from other goddesses",
      "locator": "Hestia in Art",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena tidak punya mitologi khusus, Hestia jarang muncul dalam seni Yunani dan sulit dibedakan dari dewi lain.",
        "en": "Because she has no particular mythology, Hestia appears rarely in Greek art and can be difficult to distinguish from other goddesses."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c21",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "In the Roman period, Hestia, now known as Vesta, continued to be worshipped, for example at Ephesus where the high priest was a female.",
      "locator": "Cults to the Goddess",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada masa Romawi, Hestia, yang kini dikenal sebagai Vesta, terus dipuja, misalnya di Ephesus tempat imam besarnya perempuan.",
        "en": "In the Roman period Hestia, now known as Vesta, continued to be worshipped, for example at Ephesus where the high priest was a woman."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c22",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "She appears on the celebrated Francois Vase (570-565 BCE) and, this time named, on the north frieze of the Siphinian Treasury at Delphi (c. 525 BCE) where she and an unidentified goddess face two giants in hoplite uniform.",
      "locator": "Hestia in Art",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia muncul pada Vas Francois (570-565 SM) dan, kali ini dengan nama, pada friz utara Perbendaharaan Siphnos di Delphi (sekitar 525 SM) menghadapi dua raksasa berbaju prajurit hoplit.",
        "en": "Hestia appears on the Francois Vase (570-565 BCE) and, this time named, on the north frieze of the Siphnian Treasury at Delphi (c. 525 BCE), facing two giants in hoplite uniform with an unidentified goddess."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c23",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "As the goddess of the family hearth she also presided over the cooking of bread and the preparation of the family meal.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai dewi perapian keluarga, Hestia juga memimpin pembuatan roti dan penyiapan hidangan keluarga.",
        "en": "As goddess of the family hearth, Hestia also presided over the baking of bread and the preparation of the family meal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c24",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "In myth Hestia was the first born child of Kronos (Cronus) and Rhea who was swallowed by her father at birth. Zeus later forced the old Titan to disgorge Hestia and her siblings.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitos Hestia adalah anak sulung Kronos dan Rhea yang ditelan ayahnya saat lahir; Zeus kemudian memaksa sang Titan memuntahkan Hestia dan saudara-saudaranya.",
        "en": "In myth Hestia was the firstborn of Kronos and Rhea, swallowed by her father at birth; Zeus later forced the old Titan to disgorge Hestia and her siblings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c25",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "As the first to be swallowed she was also the last to be disgorged, and so was named as both the eldest and youngest of the six Kronides.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena yang pertama ditelan sekaligus yang terakhir dimuntahkan, Hestia disebut sebagai yang tertua sekaligus termuda dari enam Kronid.",
        "en": "Being the first swallowed and last disgorged, Hestia was named both the eldest and youngest of the six Kronides."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c26",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "When the gods Apollon and Poseidon sought for her hand in marriage, Hestia refused and asked Zeus to let her remain an eternal virgin. He agreed and she took her place at his royal hearth.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketika Apollon dan Poseidon melamarnya, Hestia menolak dan meminta Zeus membiarkannya tetap perawan; Zeus setuju dan ia mengambil tempat di perapian kerajaan Zeus.",
        "en": "When Apollon and Poseidon sought her hand, Hestia refused and asked Zeus to let her remain an eternal virgin; he agreed and she took her place at his royal hearth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c27",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "So Zeus the Father gave her a high honour instead of marriage, and she has her place in the midst of the house and has the richest portion. In all the temples of the gods she has a share of honour, and among all mortal men she is chief of the goddesses.",
      "locator": "Homeric Hymn 5 to Aphrodite 18 ff",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Himne Homerik untuk Aphrodite, Zeus memberinya kehormatan tinggi sebagai ganti pernikahan: tempat di tengah rumah, bagian terkaya, bagian kehormatan di semua kuil, dan kedudukan sebagai dewi utama di antara manusia.",
        "en": "According to the Homeric Hymn to Aphrodite, Zeus gave her high honour instead of marriage: a place in the midst of the house, the richest portion, a share of honour in all temples, and the position of chief of the goddesses among mortals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c28",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "For without you mortals hold no banquet,--where one does not duly pour sweet wine in offering to Hestia both first and last.",
      "locator": "Homeric Hymn 24 to Hestia",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Himne Homerik untuk Hestia, manusia tidak mengadakan pesta tanpa Hestia, karena anggur manis dituangkan baginya lebih dulu dan terakhir.",
        "en": "According to the Homeric Hymn to Hestia, mortals hold no banquet without her, since sweet wine is poured in offering to Hestia first and last."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c29",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "Hestia was looked upon as presiding at all sacrifices, and, as the goddess of the sacred fire of the altar, she had a share in the sacrifices in all the temples of the gods.",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia dipandang memimpin semua kurban dan, sebagai dewi api suci altar, mendapat bagian dalam kurban di semua kuil para dewa.",
        "en": "Hestia was regarded as presiding at all sacrifices and, as goddess of the sacred fire of the altar, had a share in the sacrifices in all the temples of the gods."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c30",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "As the hearth was looked upon as the sacred centre of domestic life, so Hestia was the goddess of domestic life and the giver of all domestic happiness and blessings, and as such she was believed to dwell in the inner part of every house",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena perapian dipandang pusat suci kehidupan rumah tangga, Hestia dianggap dewi kehidupan rumah tangga dan pemberi segala kebahagiaan dan berkat rumah tangga, serta dipercaya berdiam di bagian dalam setiap rumah.",
        "en": "As the hearth was seen as the sacred centre of domestic life, Hestia was the goddess of domestic life and giver of all domestic happiness and blessings, believed to dwell in the inner part of every house."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c31",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "In classical sculpture she was also veiled, with a kettle as her attribute.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam patung klasik Hestia juga berkerudung, dengan ketel sebagai atributnya.",
        "en": "In classical sculpture she was also veiled, with a kettle as her attribute."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c32",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "Solemn oaths were sworn by the goddess of the hearth, and the hearth itself was the sacred asylum where suppliants implored the protection of the inhabitants of the house.",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sumpah khidmat diucapkan demi dewi perapian, dan perapian sendiri adalah suaka suci tempat pemohon meminta perlindungan penghuni rumah.",
        "en": "Solemn oaths were sworn by the goddess of the hearth, and the hearth itself was the sacred asylum where suppliants implored the protection of the household."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c33",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "Her sacrifices consisted of the primitiae of fruit, water, oil, wine, and cows of one year old.",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurban bagi Hestia berupa buah sulung, air, minyak, anggur, dan sapi berumur satu tahun.",
        "en": "Sacrifices to Hestia consisted of the first fruits, water, oil, wine, and cows one year old."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c34",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "If ever the fire of her hearth became extinct, it was not allowed to be lighted again with ordinary fire, but either by fire produced by friction, or by burning glasses drawing fire from the sun.",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jika api perapiannya padam, api itu tidak boleh dinyalakan kembali dengan api biasa, melainkan dengan api hasil gesekan atau kaca pembakar yang menarik api dari matahari.",
        "en": "If the fire of her hearth went out, it could not be relit with ordinary fire but only by fire made by friction or by burning glasses drawing fire from the sun."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c35",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "In this respect she appears often together with Hermes, who was likewise a deus penetralis, as protecting the works of man.",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia sering tampil bersama Hermes sebagai pelindung hasil karya manusia.",
        "en": "Hestia often appears together with Hermes as protector of the works of man."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c36",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "The connection between Hestia and Apollo and Poseidon, which is thus alluded to in the legend, appears also in the temple of Delphi, where the three divinities were worshipped in common, and Hestia and Poseidon appeared together also at Olympia.",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kaitan Hestia dengan Apollo dan Poseidon tampak pula di kuil Delphi, tempat ketiganya dipuja bersama, dan Hestia serta Poseidon juga muncul bersama di Olympia.",
        "en": "The link between Hestia and Apollo and Poseidon appears also in the temple at Delphi, where the three were worshipped together, and Hestia and Poseidon appeared together at Olympia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c37",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "There was, however, a separate temple of Hestia at Hermione, though it contained no image of her, but only an altar.",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada kuil Hestia tersendiri di Hermione, tetapi tanpa arca, hanya altar.",
        "en": "There was a separate temple of Hestia at Hermione, but it contained no image of her, only an altar."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c38",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "Hesiod, Theogony 453 ff (trans. Evelyn-White) (Greek epic C8th or C7th B.C.) : \"But Rhea was subject in love to Kronos (Cronus) and bare splendid children, Hestia, Demeter, and gold-shod Hera",
      "locator": "Classical literature quotes",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Theogony Hesiod (epos Yunani abad ke-8 atau ke-7 SM menurut Theoi), Rhea melahirkan anak-anak Kronos, yaitu Hestia, Demeter, dan Hera.",
        "en": "In Hesiod's Theogony (Greek epic of the 8th or 7th century BC according to Theoi), Rhea bore Kronos's children Hestia, Demeter, and Hera."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c39",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "Vesta [Hestia] lies down and takes a quiet, carefree nap, just as she was, her head pillowed by turf. But the red saviour of gardens [Priapos] prowls for Nymphai and goddesses, and wanders back and forth. He spots Vesta.",
      "locator": "Ovid, Fasti 6. 319 ff",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Fasti Ovidius, Vesta (Hestia) tertidur pulas saat pesta para dewa, dan Priapus yang berkeliaran mencari nimfa dan dewi melihatnya.",
        "en": "In Ovid's Fasti, Vesta (Hestia) takes a carefree nap during the gods' feast, and Priapus, prowling for nymphs and goddesses, spots her."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c40",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "when it bellowed an untimely bray. The goddess stars up, frightened by the noise. The whole crowd fly to her; the god flees through hostile hands.",
      "locator": "Ovid, Fasti 6. 319 ff",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kisah itu keledai Silenus meringkik pada saat yang salah; sang dewi terbangun ketakutan, para hadirin berlari menolongnya, dan Priapus lari melewati tangan-tangan yang memusuhinya.",
        "en": "In that story a donkey brays at the wrong moment; the goddess wakes frightened, the crowd rushes to her, and Priapus flees through hostile hands."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c41",
      "source_id": "hestia-s3",
      "quote": "Hestia's name means \"hearth, fireplace, altar\".",
      "locator": "Origins and etymology",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Hestia berarti \"perapian, tungku, altar\".",
        "en": "Hestia's name means \"hearth, fireplace, altar\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c42",
      "source_id": "hestia-s3",
      "quote": "the anonymous author of the hymn was trying to reconcile the two conflicting birth orders given by Hesiod and Homer; while in the Theogony the firstborn is Hestia, in the Iliad Hera states that she is the eldest child.",
      "locator": "Mythology: Origin",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Wikipedia, pengarang himne tampaknya mencoba mendamaikan dua urutan kelahiran yang bertentangan: dalam Theogony Hestia anak sulung, dalam Iliad Hera menyatakan dirinya yang tertua.",
        "en": "According to Wikipedia, the hymn's author was likely trying to reconcile two conflicting birth orders: in the Theogony Hestia is firstborn, while in the Iliad Hera states that she is the eldest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c43",
      "source_id": "hestia-s3",
      "quote": "Zeus assigned Hestia a duty to feed and maintain the fires of the Olympian hearth with the fatty, combustible portions of animal sacrifices to the gods.",
      "locator": "Mythology: Origin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Zeus menugaskan Hestia memberi makan dan memelihara api perapian Olimpus dengan bagian lemak kurban hewan yang mudah terbakar.",
        "en": "Zeus assigned Hestia the duty of feeding and maintaining the fires of the Olympian hearth with the fatty, combustible portions of animal sacrifices."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c44",
      "source_id": "hestia-s3",
      "quote": "Whenever a new colony was established, a flame from Hestia's public hearth in the mother city would be carried to the new settlement.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setiap kali koloni baru didirikan, nyala api dari perapian umum Hestia di kota induk dibawa ke permukiman baru.",
        "en": "Whenever a new colony was founded, a flame from Hestia's public hearth in the mother city was carried to the new settlement."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c45",
      "source_id": "hestia-s3",
      "quote": "Like Athena and Artemis, Hestia elected never to marry and remained an eternal virgin goddess instead, forever tending to the hearth of Olympus.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seperti Athena dan Artemis, Hestia memilih tidak pernah menikah dan tetap menjadi dewi perawan abadi yang selalu merawat perapian Olimpus.",
        "en": "Like Athena and Artemis, Hestia chose never to marry and remained an eternal virgin goddess, forever tending the hearth of Olympus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c46",
      "source_id": "hestia-s3",
      "quote": "Ancient Greek: Ἑστία, lit. 'hearth, fireplace, altar'",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bahasa Yunani Kuno namanya ditulis Ἑστία, secara harfiah \"perapian, tungku, altar\".",
        "en": "In Ancient Greek her name is written Ἑστία, literally 'hearth, fireplace, altar'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c47",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "Nor yet does the pure maiden Histia (Hestia) love Aphrodite's works.",
      "locator": "Homeric Hymn 5 to Aphrodite 18 ff",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Himne Homerik untuk Aphrodite namanya ditulis Histia (Hestia).",
        "en": "In the Homeric Hymn to Aphrodite her name is written Histia (Hestia)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c48",
      "source_id": "hestia-s2",
      "quote": "where the goddess had her especial sanctuary (thalamos), under the name of Prutanitis, with a statue and the sacred hearth.",
      "locator": "Encyclopedia (Smith, Dictionary of Greek and Roman Biography and Mythology)",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di prytaneum kota, Hestia memiliki tempat suci khusus dengan nama Prutanitis, lengkap dengan arca dan perapian suci.",
        "en": "In a town's prytaneum Hestia had her special sanctuary under the name Prutanitis, with a statue and the sacred hearth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c49",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "Priests and priestesses specifically dedicated to Hestia seem to have been particularly prevalent from the Hellenistic period.",
      "locator": "Cults to the Goddess",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Imam dan imam perempuan yang khusus mengabdi kepada Hestia tampaknya sangat lazim sejak masa Helenistik.",
        "en": "Priests and priestesses specifically dedicated to Hestia seem to have been particularly prevalent from the Hellenistic period."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c50",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "For this reason, there developed a saying 'to begin from Hestia.'",
      "locator": "The Hearth in Greek Culture",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena Hestia disebut pertama, berkembang ungkapan \"mulai dari Hestia\".",
        "en": "Because Hestia was named first, a saying developed: 'to begin from Hestia.'"
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hestia-c51",
      "source_id": "hestia-s1",
      "quote": "sometimes she is pouring a libation, as in a c. 500 BCE red-figure kylix now in the Berlin Staatliche Museen which shows her in the company of Apollo and Hermes as they lead Hercules to Mount Olympus.",
      "locator": "Hestia in Art",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hestia kadang digambarkan menuang persembahan, misalnya pada kylix figur merah sekitar 500 SM di Berlin bersama Apollo dan Hermes yang menuntun Hercules ke Gunung Olimpus.",
        "en": "Hestia is sometimes shown pouring a libation, as on a c. 500 BCE red-figure kylix in Berlin where she is with Apollo and Hermes as they lead Hercules to Mount Olympus."
      }
    }
  ],

    {

      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Satu sumber menyebut Apollo, Poseidon, dan Priapus yang memperhatikannya.",
            "en": "One source names Apollo, Poseidon, and Priapus as paying her attention."
          },

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Isis adalah dewi Mesir kuno, istri Osiris dan ibu Horus, yang dikenal karena kuasa sihirnya dan perlindungannya, dan pemujaannya menyebar ke dunia Yunani-Romawi.",
    "en": "Isis is an ancient Egyptian goddess, wife of Osiris and mother of Horus, known for her magical power and protection, whose worship spread throughout the Greco-Roman world.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Isis adalah dewi Mesir kuno yang menjadi paling populer dan paling lestari di antara dewa-dewa Mesir. Ia istri Osiris dan ibu Horus, sehingga secara simbolis menjadi ibu bagi firaun. Namanya pertama tercatat dalam Teks Piramida dari Dinasti Kelima. Kultusnya bermula di Delta Nil, dengan tempat suci terpenting di Behbeit El-Hagar, lalu menyebar ke seluruh Mesir.",
      "en": "Isis is an ancient Egyptian goddess who rose to be the most popular and long-lasting of the Egyptian deities. She is the wife of Osiris and the mother of Horus, and so symbolically the mother of the pharaoh. Her name is first attested in the Pyramid Texts of the Fifth Dynasty. Her cult began in the Nile Delta, with its most important sanctuary at Behbeit El-Hagar, and spread to all parts of Egypt.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "isis-c01",
        "isis-c03",
        "isis-c02",
        "isis-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitos Osiris, Set iri kepada saudaranya dan membuat peti mati seukuran tubuh Osiris. Isis mencari suaminya ke seluruh Mesir, membawa jasadnya kembali, lalu menghidupkannya; dalam wujud kite, sejenis elang, ia terbang mengelilinginya dan mengandung Horus. Osiris kemudian mundur ke alam baka sebagai Penguasa dan Hakim Orang Mati, sementara Isis menyembunyikan Horus di rawa-rawa sampai dewasa. Horus kemudian melawan Set demi menguasai dunia.",
      "en": "In the myth of Osiris, Set was jealous of his brother and had a coffin made to Osiris's exact height. Isis searched all through Egypt for her husband, brought his body back, and revived him; in the form of a kite, a kind of falcon, she flew around him and became pregnant with Horus. Osiris then withdrew to the afterlife as Lord and Judge of the Dead, while Isis hid Horus in the swamps until he was grown. Horus then battled Set for control of the world.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "isis-c17",
        "isis-c18",
        "isis-c19",
        "isis-c20",
        "isis-c21",
        "isis-c22"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Isis dikenal karena sihirnya. Kuasa sihirnya konon melampaui semua dewa lain, dan ia dikatakan mengatur alam serta berkuasa atas takdir; ia juga disebut Weret-Kekau, \"Sihir Agung\". Dalam sebuah mantra ia membuat Ra sakit dengan gigitan ular dan menawarkan penyembuhan jika Ra mengungkapkan nama rahasianya. Dalam kisah Isis dan Tujuh Kalajengking, ia menyembuhkan seorang anak dengan memanggil nama rahasia para kalajengking dan melafalkan mantra sihir agung. Bantuan keibuannya juga dipanggil dalam mantra penyembuhan bagi orang biasa.",
      "en": "Isis was known for her magic. Her magic was reputed to surpass that of every other god, and she was said to rule the natural world and hold sway over fate; she was also called Weret-Kekau, \"the Great Magic\". In one spell she makes Ra ill with a snake's bite and offers to cure him if he reveals his secret name. In the story of Isis and the Seven Scorpions she heals a child by calling the scorpions by their secret names and reciting spells of great magic. Her maternal aid was also invoked in healing spells for ordinary people.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "isis-c11",
        "isis-c42",
        "isis-c43",
        "isis-c23",
        "isis-c45"

    {
      "id": "Ketika Aleksander Agung menaklukkan Mesir pada 331 SM, pasukannya mengaitkan Isis dengan Demeter. Di Roma pemujaannya dikukuhkan pada masa Caligula, dan kuil Isis ditemukan antara lain di Pompeii dan Londinium. Dalam karya Apuleius (terjemahan Adlington), sang dewi menyebut dirinya ibu alami segala sesuatu dan Ratu Langit, dan menyatakan bahwa orang Mesir menyebutnya Ratu Isis. Kuil Isis di Philae tetap menjadi tempat ziarah hingga ditutup oleh kaisar Justinian pada 529 M.",
      "en": "When Alexander the Great conquered Egypt in 331 BCE his troops associated Isis with Demeter. In Rome her worship was firmly established under Caligula, and temples of Isis have been found at Pompeii and Londinium, among other places. In Apuleius's work (Adlington's translation) the goddess calls herself the natural mother of all things and Queen of heaven, and says the Egyptians call her Queen Isis. Her temple at Philae remained a pilgrimage site until the emperor Justinian closed it in 529 CE.",
      "claim_ids": [

      "id": "Seiring waktu Isis menjadi begitu populer sehingga semua dewa dianggap sekadar aspek Isis, dan ia satu-satunya dewa Mesir yang dipuja semua orang. Kultusnya tumbuh menjadi agama misteri yang menjanjikan rahasia hidup dan mati kepada para peserta inisiasi, ia dikaitkan dengan laut sebagai pelindung pelaut dan pedagang, dan orang Yunani serta Romawi memuliakannya sebagai dewa tertinggi pencipta dunia.",
      "en": "Over time Isis grew so popular that all gods were regarded as mere aspects of her, and she was the one Egyptian deity worshipped by everyone.       },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Dalam terjemahan Adlington atas karya Apuleius, sang dewi menampakkan diri dengan rebana kuningan dan cawan emas, menyebut dirinya ibu alami segala sesuatu, dan menyatakan bahwa ia dipuja dengan banyak nama, tetapi orang Mesir menyebutnya Ratu Isis.",
        "en": "In Adlington's translation of Apuleius, the goddess appears with a brass timbrel and a golden cup, calls herself the natural mother of all things, and says she is worshipped under many names, but that the Egyptians call her Queen Isis."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "isis-c40",
        "isis-c36",
        "isis-c38",
        "isis-c39"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "timeline": [

  "modern_depictions": [],
  "tradition_vs_modern": {
    "traditional": {
      "id": "Dalam tradisi Mesir kuno, Isis adalah istri Osiris dan ibu Horus yang menghidupkan suaminya dan melindungi putranya.",
      "en": "In ancient Egyptian tradition Isis is the wife of Osiris and mother of Horus who revived her husband and protected her son."
    },
    "modern": {
      "id": "Menurut Wikipedia, Isis masih muncul dalam budaya Barat, terutama dalam esoterisme dan paganisme modern, sering sebagai personifikasi alam atau sisi feminin keilahian.",
      "en": "According to Wikipedia, Isis still appears in Western culture, especially in esotericism and modern paganism, often as a personification of nature or the feminine aspect of divinity."
    },
    "claim_ids": [
      "isis-c03",
      "isis-c04",
      "isis-c51"
    ]
  },
  "learning_questions": [
    {
      "id": "Mengapa kisah Isis mencari Osiris dan melindungi Horus begitu berpengaruh bagi orang Mesir kuno?",
      "en": "Why was the story of Isis searching for Osiris and protecting Horus so influential for the ancient Egyptians?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Apa yang membuat dewi Mesir ini dapat dipuja di Yunani dan Roma?",
      "en": "What allowed this Egyptian goddess to be worshipped in Greece and Rome?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Bagaimana kita membedakan apa yang dikatakan tradisi Mesir dari apa yang dikatakan karya sastra Romawi tentang Isis?",
      "en": "How can we tell what Egyptian tradition says about Isis apart from what a Roman literary work says?"
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "isis-c01",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Isis is an ancient Egyptian goddess who became the most popular and enduring of all the Egyptian deities.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis adalah dewi Mesir kuno.",
        "en": "Isis is an ancient Egyptian goddess."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c02",
      "source_id": "isis-s2",
      "quote": "Isis’ name is first attested in the fifth dynasty in the Pyramid texts.",
      "locator": "Object description",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Isis pertama kali tercatat dalam Teks Piramida dari dinasti kelima.",
        "en": "Isis's name is first attested in the Fifth Dynasty Pyramid Texts."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c03",
      "source_id": "isis-s2",
      "quote": "She was the wife of Osiris and the mother of Horus, and thus was symbolically mother to the pharaoh.",
      "locator": "Object description",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis adalah istri Osiris dan ibu Horus.",
        "en": "Isis is the wife of Osiris and mother of Horus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c04",
      "source_id": "isis-s3",
      "quote": "According to this myth Isis was able to revive Osiris after he had been killed by his brother Seth and she became pregnant with their son Horus.",
      "locator": "Object description",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut mitos, Isis menghidupkan kembali Osiris setelah dibunuh Seth.",
        "en": "According to the myth, Isis revived Osiris after Seth killed him."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c05",
      "source_id": "isis-s3",
      "quote": "After Horus’ birth, Isis nurtured her child and protected him from Seth and other dangers.",
      "locator": "Object description",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis membesarkan Horus dan melindunginya dari Seth.",
        "en": "Isis nurtured Horus and protected him from Seth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c06",
      "source_id": "isis-s2",
      "quote": "This statuette shows the goddess in her most beloved pose, nursing her son Horus (known also as the lactans pose).",
      "locator": "Object description",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebuah patung kecil menunjukkan Isis menyusui putranya Horus.",
        "en": "A statuette shows Isis nursing Horus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c07",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Her cult began in the Nile Delta and her most important sanctuary was there at the shrine of Behbeit El-Hagar, but worship of Isis eventually spread to all parts of Egypt.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kultus Isis bermula di Delta Nil dan akhirnya menyebar ke seluruh Mesir.",
        "en": "Isis's cult began in the Nile Delta and spread throughout Egypt."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c08",
      "source_id": "isis-s2",
      "quote": "The abundance of statuettes in this particular pose demonstrate some of the qualities for which Isis was most valued in the first millennium BC: her role as a life-giver and protector.",
      "locator": "Object description",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Patung-patung Isis bersama Horus menonjolkan perannya sebagai pemberi kehidupan dan pelindung.",
        "en": "Statuettes of Isis and Horus emphasize her role as life-giver and protector."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c09",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Her name comes from the Egyptian Eset, (\"the seat\") which referred to her stability and also the throne of Egypt as she was considered the mother of every pharaoh through the king's association with Horus, Isis' son.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut World History Encyclopedia, namanya berasal dari kata Mesir Eset (\"tempat duduk\"), yang merujuk pada kestabilannya dan juga takhta Mesir, karena ia dianggap ibu setiap firaun lewat kaitan raja dengan Horus, putranya.",
        "en": "According to World History Encyclopedia, her name comes from the Egyptian Eset (\"the seat\"), referring to her stability and also the throne of Egypt, as she was considered the mother of every pharaoh through the king's association with Horus, her son."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c10",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Her symbols are the scorpion (who kept her safe when she was in hiding), the kite (a kind of falcon whose shape she assumed in bringing her husband back to life), the empty throne, and the sistrum.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lambang Isis adalah kalajengking (yang menjaganya saat bersembunyi), kite (sejenis elang yang wujudnya ia ambil untuk menghidupkan suaminya), takhta kosong, dan sistrum.",
        "en": "Isis's symbols are the scorpion (who kept her safe in hiding), the kite (a kind of falcon whose shape she assumed in bringing her husband back to life), the empty throne, and the sistrum."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c11",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "She was also known as Weret-Kekau (\"the Great Magic\") for her power and Mut-Netjer, \"Mother of the Gods\" but was known by many names depending on which role she was fulfilling at the moment.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis juga dikenal sebagai Weret-Kekau (\"Sihir Agung\") karena kekuatannya dan Mut-Netjer, \"Ibu Para Dewa\", serta punya banyak nama menurut peran yang sedang dijalankannya.",
        "en": "Isis was also known as Weret-Kekau (\"the Great Magic\") for her power and Mut-Netjer, \"Mother of the Gods\", and had many names depending on the role she was fulfilling."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c12",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "In time, she became so popular that all gods were considered mere aspects of Isis and she was the only Egyptian deity worshiped by everyone in the country.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lama-kelamaan Isis begitu populer sehingga semua dewa dianggap sekadar aspek Isis dan ia satu-satunya dewa Mesir yang dipuja semua orang di negeri itu.",
        "en": "In time Isis became so popular that all gods were considered mere aspects of her and she was the only Egyptian deity worshiped by everyone in the country."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c13",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Like the Eleusinian Mysteries, the Cult of Isis grew into a Mystery Religion promising the secrets of life and death to initiates, who were then sworn to secrecy.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seperti Misteri Eleusis, kultus Isis tumbuh menjadi agama misteri yang menjanjikan rahasia hidup dan mati kepada para peserta inisiasi, yang disumpah merahasiakannya.",
        "en": "Like the Eleusinian Mysteries, the cult of Isis grew into a mystery religion promising the secrets of life and death to initiates, who were sworn to secrecy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c14",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Her temple on the island of Philae in Upper Egypt would remain an active pilgrimage site for thousands of years until closed in the 6th century CE by the Christian emperor Justinian.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuil Isis di pulau Philae di Mesir Hulu tetap menjadi tempat ziarah selama ribuan tahun hingga ditutup pada abad ke-6 M oleh kaisar Kristen Justinian.",
        "en": "Her temple on the island of Philae in Upper Egypt remained an active pilgrimage site for thousands of years until closed in the 6th century CE by the Christian emperor Justinian."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c15",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Eventually, she became associated with the sea and was a protectress of sailors and merchants who wore talismans honoring her and invoked her aid in times of trouble (attested to by archaeological evidence).",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis akhirnya dikaitkan dengan laut dan menjadi pelindung pelaut dan pedagang yang memakai jimat untuknya dan memohon bantuannya saat bahaya.",
        "en": "Isis eventually became associated with the sea and was protectress of sailors and merchants who wore talismans honoring her and invoked her aid in times of trouble."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c16",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Unlike the other gods of Egypt, Isis transcended national borders and was worshiped by the Greeks and the Romans who believed in her as the supreme deity who created the world.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berbeda dari dewa Mesir lainnya, Isis melampaui batas negara dan dipuja orang Yunani dan Romawi yang meyakininya sebagai dewa tertinggi pencipta dunia.",
        "en": "Unlike other Egyptian gods, Isis transcended national borders and was worshiped by Greeks and Romans, who believed in her as the supreme deity who created the world."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c17",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Set was jealous of his brother's power and prestige, however, and so conceived a plan to get rid of him. He had a beautiful coffin made to Osiris' exact height",
      "locator": "Osiris and Set",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Set iri pada kekuasaan dan gengsi saudaranya dan merencanakan menyingkirkannya, dengan membuat peti mati indah seukuran tinggi Osiris.",
        "en": "Set was jealous of his brother's power and prestige and plotted to get rid of him, having a beautiful coffin made to Osiris's exact height."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c18",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Isis was distraught when she found her husband was missing and went searching for him all through Egypt without success.",
      "locator": "Osiris and Set",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis sangat bersedih ketika suaminya hilang dan mencarinya ke seluruh Mesir tanpa hasil.",
        "en": "Isis was distraught when her husband went missing and searched all through Egypt without success."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c19",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "After leaving the court, Isis cut Osiris from the tree and carried his body back to Egypt where she hid him from Set in the swampy region of the Nile Delta.",
      "locator": "Resurrection of Osiris",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis mengeluarkan Osiris dari pohon dan membawa jasadnya kembali ke Mesir, menyembunyikannya dari Set di rawa-rawa Delta Nil.",
        "en": "Isis cut Osiris from the tree and carried his body back to Egypt, hiding him from Set in the swampy region of the Nile Delta."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c20",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Isis still was able to revive Osiris and, once he was alive, she assumed the form of a kite and flew around him, drew the seed from his body into her own, and became pregnant with a son, Horus",
      "locator": "Resurrection of Osiris",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis berhasil menghidupkan kembali Osiris, mengambil wujud kite (sejenis elang), terbang mengelilinginya, dan mengandung putra, Horus.",
        "en": "Isis was still able to revive Osiris and, in the form of a kite, flew around him and became pregnant with a son, Horus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c21",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Even though Osiris now lived, he was incomplete and could no longer rule the land of the living. He withdrew into the afterlife where he became Lord and Judge of the Dead.",
      "locator": "Resurrection of Osiris",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski hidup lagi, Osiris tidak lengkap dan tak bisa lagi memerintah dunia orang hidup; ia mundur ke alam baka dan menjadi Penguasa dan Hakim Orang Mati.",
        "en": "Though alive again, Osiris was incomplete and could no longer rule the living; he withdrew into the afterlife where he became Lord and Judge of the Dead."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c22",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Isis, fearing what Set might do to her son, hid Horus among the swamps of Egypt until he was grown. At that point, Horus emerged as a mighty warrior and battled Set for control of the world.",
      "locator": "Resurrection of Osiris",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena takut akan perbuatan Set, Isis menyembunyikan Horus di rawa-rawa Mesir sampai dewasa; Horus lalu muncul sebagai prajurit perkasa dan melawan Set demi menguasai dunia.",
        "en": "Fearing what Set might do, Isis hid Horus in the swamps of Egypt until he was grown; Horus then emerged as a mighty warrior and battled Set for control of the world."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c23",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Isis took the child in her arms and called each of the scorpions by their secret name, thereby dominating them and neutralizing their power, and recited spells of great magic. The poison evaporated, leaving the child's body, and he revived.",
      "locator": "Isis and Harmony",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kisah Isis dan Tujuh Kalajengking, Isis memanggil tiap kalajengking dengan nama rahasianya sehingga menundukkan mereka, melafalkan mantra sihir agung, dan racun lenyap dari tubuh anak itu sehingga ia sadar kembali.",
        "en": "In the story of Isis and the Seven Scorpions, Isis called each scorpion by its secret name, dominating them, and recited spells of great magic; the poison left the child's body and he revived."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c24",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "The story relates how, when Horus was an infant and Isis was hiding him in the swamp lands from Set, seven scorpions kept her company. She would sometimes go out in the evenings for food, and the scorpions would form a kind of bodyguard around her.",
      "locator": "Isis and Harmony",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketika Horus masih bayi dan Isis menyembunyikannya di rawa dari Set, tujuh kalajengking menemaninya dan membentuk semacam pengawal di sekelilingnya saat ia keluar mencari makan pada malam hari.",
        "en": "When Horus was an infant and Isis was hiding him in the swamp from Set, seven scorpions kept her company and formed a kind of bodyguard around her when she went out for food in the evenings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c25",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "When Alexander the Great (l. 356-323 BCE) conquered Egypt in 331 BCE his troops associated Isis with the popular Greek goddess Demeter.",
      "locator": "Isis Outside Egypt",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketika Aleksander Agung menaklukkan Mesir pada 331 SM, pasukannya mengaitkan Isis dengan dewi Yunani Demeter.",
        "en": "When Alexander the Great conquered Egypt in 331 BCE his troops associated Isis with the Greek goddess Demeter."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c26",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "under the reign of Caligula (r. 37-41 CE) the worship of Isis in Rome was firmly established by the emperor through the Isiac Festival in her honor.",
      "locator": "Isis Outside Egypt",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada masa Caligula (37-41 M) pemujaan Isis di Roma dikukuhkan oleh kaisar lewat Festival Isiak.",
        "en": "Under Caligula (37-41 CE) the worship of Isis in Rome was firmly established by the emperor through the Isiac Festival in her honor."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c27",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "A temple of Isis has been found in Pompeii, and another in Great Britain at the Roman site of Londinium (modern London).",
      "locator": "Isis Outside Egypt",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuil Isis ditemukan di Pompeii dan di situs Romawi Londinium (London modern) di Britania Raya.",
        "en": "A temple of Isis has been found in Pompeii, and another at the Roman site of Londinium (modern London) in Great Britain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c28",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Her cult in Rome was the greatest rival to the young religion of Christianity, which drew upon the image of Isis and the child-god Horus for the depiction of the Madonna with the Christ child.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut World History Encyclopedia, kultus Isis di Roma adalah saingan terbesar agama Kristen yang masih muda, yang meminjam citra Isis dan Horus kecil untuk penggambaran Madonna dengan bayi Kristus.",
        "en": "According to World History Encyclopedia, her cult in Rome was the greatest rival of the young Christian religion, which drew on the image of Isis and the child-god Horus for the Madonna with the Christ child."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c29",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Philae continued as a vital site dedicated to Isis until the emperor Justinian closed it, along with other important centers of learning and worship in 529 CE.",
      "locator": "Isis and Christianity",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Philae tetap menjadi situs penting bagi Isis hingga kaisar Justinian menutupnya pada 529 M.",
        "en": "Philae continued as a vital site dedicated to Isis until the emperor Justinian closed it in 529 CE."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c30",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Nephthys was never depicted as evil to Isis' good but was associated with death while Isis with life and so these two sister-deities symbolized cosmic balance.",
      "locator": "Isis and Harmony",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nephthys tidak pernah digambarkan jahat bagi Isis yang baik, melainkan dikaitkan dengan kematian sedangkan Isis dengan kehidupan, sehingga kedua dewi bersaudari itu melambangkan keseimbangan kosmis.",
        "en": "Nephthys was never depicted as evil to Isis's good but was associated with death while Isis was associated with life, so the two sister-deities symbolized cosmic balance."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c31",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "She and her husband and son replaced the Theban Triad of Amon, Mut, and Khons, who had been the most popular trinity of gods in Egypt.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis bersama suami dan putranya menggantikan Triad Thebes (Amon, Mut, dan Khons), trinitas dewa yang paling populer di Mesir.",
        "en": "Isis, her husband, and her son replaced the Theban Triad of Amon, Mut, and Khons, the most popular trinity of gods in Egypt."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c32",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "As the goddess who brought the yearly inundation of the Nile which fertilized the land she was Sati, for example, and as the goddess who created and preserved life she was Ankhet, and so on.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai dewi pembawa banjir tahunan Sungai Nil yang menyuburkan tanah ia disebut Sati, dan sebagai dewi yang menciptakan dan memelihara kehidupan ia disebut Ankhet.",
        "en": "As the goddess who brought the yearly Nile inundation that fertilized the land she was Sati, and as the goddess who created and preserved life she was Ankhet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c33",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "She is regularly portrayed as the selfless, giving, mother, wife, and protectress, who places other's interests and well-being ahead of her own.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis biasanya digambarkan sebagai ibu, istri, dan pelindung yang tanpa pamrih, murah hati, dan mengutamakan kepentingan orang lain.",
        "en": "Isis is regularly portrayed as a selfless, giving mother, wife, and protectress who puts others' interests and well-being ahead of her own."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c34",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Nut, however, was already pregnant by Geb and soon gave birth to five children: Osiris, Isis, Set, Nephthys, and Horus (known as Horus the Elder).",
      "locator": "Mythical Origin",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nut melahirkan lima anak dari Geb: Osiris, Isis, Set, Nephthys, dan Horus (Horus yang Tua).",
        "en": "Nut gave birth to five children by Geb: Osiris, Isis, Set, Nephthys, and Horus (known as Horus the Elder)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c35",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Osiris married Isis and, as the first born, assumed rule as Lord of the Earth, with Isis as his queen and consort.",
      "locator": "Osiris and Set",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Osiris menikahi Isis dan, sebagai anak sulung, memerintah sebagai Penguasa Bumi dengan Isis sebagai ratu dan pendampingnya.",
        "en": "Osiris married Isis and, as the firstborn, ruled as Lord of the Earth with Isis as his queen and consort."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c36",
      "source_id": "isis-s4",
      "quote": "I am she that is the naturall mother of all things, mistresse and governesse of all the Elements, the initiall progeny of worlds, chiefe of powers divine, Queene of heaven!",
      "locator": "Book 11, the goddess speaks to Lucius",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam terjemahan Adlington atas karya Apuleius, sang dewi menyebut dirinya ibu alami segala sesuatu, penguasa semua unsur, keturunan awal dunia, pemimpin kuasa ilahi, dan Ratu Langit.",
        "en": "In Adlington's translation of Apuleius, the goddess calls herself the natural mother of all things, mistress of all the elements, the initial progeny of worlds, chief of divine powers, and Queen of heaven."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c37",
      "source_id": "isis-s4",
      "quote": "at my will the planets of the ayre, the wholesome winds of the Seas, and the silences of hell be diposed",
      "locator": "Book 11, the goddess speaks to Lucius",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sang dewi dalam karya Apuleius menyatakan bahwa atas kehendaknya planet-planet langit, angin laut yang menyehatkan, dan kesunyian neraka diatur.",
        "en": "In Apuleius's work the goddess declares that at her will the planets of the air, the wholesome winds of the seas, and the silences of hell are disposed."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c38",
      "source_id": "isis-s4",
      "quote": "for the Phrygians call me the mother of the Gods: the Athenians, Minerva: the Cyprians, Venus: the Candians, Diana: the Sicilians Proserpina: the Eleusians, Ceres: some Juno, other Bellona, other Hecate",
      "locator": "Book 11, the goddess speaks to Lucius",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam karya Apuleius sang dewi menyebut bahwa ia dipuja dengan banyak nama: bangsa Frigia menyebutnya ibu para dewa, orang Athena Minerva, orang Siprus Venus, dan seterusnya.",
        "en": "In Apuleius's work the goddess says she is worshipped under many names: the Phrygians call her mother of the Gods, the Athenians Minerva, the Cyprians Venus, and so on."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c39",
      "source_id": "isis-s4",
      "quote": "and the Aegyptians which are excellent in all kind of ancient doctrine, and by their proper ceremonies accustome to worship mee, doe call mee Queene Isis.",
      "locator": "Book 11, the goddess speaks to Lucius",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam karya Apuleius, orang Mesir, yang unggul dalam segala ajaran kuno dan memujanya dengan upacara mereka sendiri, menyebutnya Ratu Isis.",
        "en": "In Apuleius's work the Egyptians, excellent in all ancient doctrine and worshipping her with their own ceremonies, call her Queen Isis."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c40",
      "source_id": "isis-s4",
      "quote": "In her right hand shee had a timbrell of brasse, which gave a pleasant sound, in her left hand shee bare a cup of gold, out of the mouth whereof the serpent Aspis lifted up his head",
      "locator": "Book 11, the vision of the goddess",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam penglihatan di karya Apuleius, sang dewi memegang rebana kuningan di tangan kanan dan cawan emas di tangan kiri, dengan ular aspis mengangkat kepala dari mulut cawan.",
        "en": "In the vision in Apuleius's work, the goddess holds a brass timbrel in her right hand and a golden cup in her left, from whose mouth a serpent, the asp, lifts its head."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c41",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "Isis was a major goddess in ancient Egyptian religion whose worship spread throughout the Greco-Roman world.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis adalah dewi utama dalam agama Mesir kuno yang pemujaannya menyebar ke seluruh dunia Yunani-Romawi.",
        "en": "Isis was a major goddess in ancient Egyptian religion whose worship spread throughout the Greco-Roman world."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c42",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "Her reputed magical power was greater than that of all other gods, and she was said to govern the natural world and wield power over fate itself.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kekuatan sihirnya konon lebih besar daripada semua dewa lain, dan ia dikatakan mengatur alam dan berkuasa atas takdir.",
        "en": "Her reputed magical power was greater than that of all other gods, and she was said to govern the natural world and wield power over fate itself."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c43",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "In one spell, Isis creates a snake that bites Ra, who is older and greater than she is, and makes him ill with its venom. She offers to cure Ra if he will tell her his true, secret name",
      "locator": "Roles",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam salah satu mantra, Isis menciptakan ular yang menggigit Ra dan membuatnya sakit dengan bisanya, lalu menawarkan menyembuhkannya jika Ra mau menyebut nama rahasianya yang sejati.",
        "en": "In one spell Isis creates a snake that bites Ra and makes him ill with its venom, then offers to cure him if he tells her his true, secret name."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c44",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "In later texts, she uses her powers of transformation to fight and destroy Set and his followers.",
      "locator": "Roles",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam teks-teks kemudian, Isis memakai kekuatan perubahan wujudnya untuk melawan dan menghancurkan Set dan para pengikutnya.",
        "en": "In later texts Isis uses her powers of transformation to fight and destroy Set and his followers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c45",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "She was believed to help the dead enter the afterlife as she had helped Osiris, and she was considered the divine mother of the pharaoh, who was likened to Horus. Her maternal aid was invoked in healing spells to benefit ordinary people.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis dipercaya membantu orang mati memasuki alam baka seperti ia menolong Osiris, dianggap ibu ilahi firaun, dan bantuannya dipanggil dalam mantra penyembuhan bagi orang biasa.",
        "en": "Isis was believed to help the dead enter the afterlife as she had helped Osiris, was considered the divine mother of the pharaoh, and her maternal aid was invoked in healing spells for ordinary people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c46",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "neither Isis nor her husband Osiris were mentioned by name before the Fifth Dynasty (c. 2494-2345 BCE).",
      "locator": "Name and origins",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Baik Isis maupun suaminya Osiris tidak disebut namanya sebelum Dinasti Kelima (sekitar 2494-2345 SM).",
        "en": "Neither Isis nor her husband Osiris were mentioned by name before the Fifth Dynasty (c. 2494-2345 BCE)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c47",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "During the New Kingdom (c. 1550 - c. 1070 BCE), Isis took on traits that originally belonged to Hathor, the preeminent goddess of earlier time.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Kerajaan Baru (sekitar 1550-1070 SM), Isis mengambil sifat yang semula milik Hathor, dewi terkemuka masa sebelumnya.",
        "en": "During the New Kingdom (c. 1550-1070 BCE) Isis took on traits that originally belonged to Hathor, the preeminent goddess of an earlier time."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c48",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "Her Egyptian name was written as 𓊨𓏏𓐰𓆇𓁐 (ꜣst)",
      "locator": "Name and origins",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Mesir Isis ditulis 𓊨𓏏𓐰𓆇𓁐 (ꜣst).",
        "en": "Isis's Egyptian name was written 𓊨𓏏𓐰𓆇𓁐 (ꜣst)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c49",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "Therefore, the Egyptologist Kurt Sethe suggested she was originally a personification of thrones.",
      "locator": "Name and origins",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Egiptolog Kurt Sethe mengusulkan bahwa Isis semula adalah personifikasi takhta.",
        "en": "The Egyptologist Kurt Sethe suggested she was originally a personification of thrones."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c50",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "Other scholars, such as Jürgen Osing and Klaus P. Kuhlmann, have disputed this interpretation, because of dissimilarities between Isis's name and the word for a throne or a lack of evidence that the throne was ever deified.",
      "locator": "Name and origins",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sarjana lain, seperti Jürgen Osing dan Klaus P. Kuhlmann, menolak tafsir itu karena perbedaan antara nama Isis dan kata untuk takhta, atau kurangnya bukti bahwa takhta pernah diilahikan.",
        "en": "Other scholars, such as Jürgen Osing and Klaus P. Kuhlmann, dispute this interpretation because of dissimilarities between Isis's name and the word for a throne, or a lack of evidence that the throne was ever deified."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c51",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "Isis continues to appear in Western culture, particularly in esotericism and modern paganism, often as a personification of nature or the feminine aspect of divinity.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis terus muncul dalam budaya Barat, khususnya dalam esoterisme dan paganisme modern, sering sebagai personifikasi alam atau sisi feminin keilahian.",
        "en": "Isis continues to appear in Western culture, particularly in esotericism and modern paganism, often as a personification of nature or the feminine aspect of divinity."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c52",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "Isis's Greek devotees ascribed to her traits taken from Greek deities, such as the invention of marriage and the protection of ships at sea.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para pemuja Yunani Isis mengaitkan dengannya sifat dari dewa Yunani, seperti penemuan pernikahan dan perlindungan kapal di laut.",
        "en": "Isis's Greek devotees ascribed to her traits taken from Greek deities, such as the invention of marriage and the protection of ships at sea."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c53",
      "source_id": "isis-s5",
      "quote": "Her worship may have influenced Christian beliefs and practices such as the veneration of Mary, but the evidence for this influence is ambiguous and often controversial.",
      "locator": "Lead",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Wikipedia, pemujaan Isis mungkin memengaruhi kepercayaan dan praktik Kristen seperti penghormatan kepada Maria, tetapi bukti pengaruh itu tidak jelas dan sering diperdebatkan.",
        "en": "According to Wikipedia, her worship may have influenced Christian beliefs and practices such as the veneration of Mary, but the evidence for this influence is ambiguous and often controversial."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "isis-c54",
      "source_id": "isis-s1",
      "quote": "Isis became the goddess of all the people of Egypt, male and female, royal and common, alike.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Isis menjadi dewi seluruh rakyat Mesir, laki-laki dan perempuan, bangsawan dan rakyat biasa.",
        "en": "Isis became the goddess of all the people of Egypt, male and female, royal and common alike."
      }
    }
  ],

}

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Kali adalah dewi Hindu kematian, waktu, dan akhir zaman yang dalam seni tampil menakutkan, tetapi juga dipuja sebagai sosok ibu.",
    "en": "Kali is the Hindu goddess of death, time, and doomsday, fearsome in art but also worshipped as a mother figure.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kali-c01",
